```sh
    # 4) Re-check right before deleting.
    if [ "$(ubu_count_mounts_under "$_canon")" != 0 ]; then
        ubu_log "REFUSED to delete $_canon: a mount appeared under it"
        return 3
    fi

    ubu_log "deleting $_canon"
    bb rm -rf "$_canon"
}

# ubu_ensure_profile_hook ROOTFS: login hook that applies UBU_CWD / UBU_INIT
# (start directory and initial command chosen by the app), then unsets them.
ubu_ensure_profile_hook() {
    [ -f "$1/etc/profile.d/uburoot.sh" ] && return 0
    ubu_write_profile_hook "$1"
}

# Always (re)writes it: setup.sh and its migrations.
ubu_write_profile_hook() {
    _f="$1/etc/profile.d/uburoot.sh"
    mkdir -p "$1/etc/profile.d" || return 1
    cat >"$_f" <<'EOF'
# UbuRoot login hook: start directory and initial command chosen by the app.
if [ -n "${UBU_CWD:-}" ]; then
    cd "$UBU_CWD" 2>/dev/null || cd "$HOME" 2>/dev/null || cd /
fi
unset UBU_CWD
if [ -n "${UBU_INIT:-}" ]; then
    _ubu_init=$UBU_INIT
    unset UBU_INIT
    eval "$_ubu_init"
    unset _ubu_init
fi
EOF
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Processes chrooted into the rootfs (their /proc/PID/root points inside it).
# One ls + awk instead of a readlink per process: ~600 forks took ~12 s when
# run from the app, whose root shells share its (background-limited) cgroup.
ubu_chroot_pids() {
    # shellcheck disable=SC2012 # symlink targets of /proc/*/root: no odd file names here
    ls -l /proc/[0-9]*/root 2>/dev/null | bb awk -v r="$ROOTFS" '
        $(NF - 1) == "->" && ($NF == r || index($NF, r "/") == 1) {
            split($(NF - 2), a, "/")
            print a[3]
        }'
}

ubu_init() {
    ubu_detect_busybox
    ubu_validate_paths
}

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/doctor.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# uburoot-doctor: what UbuRoot needs from this phone and what it found.
# Usage: doctor.sh [--redact] [--app-version V]
#   --redact  replace identifiers (IP addresses, user names, host names, serial
#             numbers) with <redacted>; the app shares this version by default.
# Read-only: it mounts, installs and changes nothing. The DNS and mirror checks
# run inside Ubuntu, so only while it is mounted.
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_require_root
ubu_init
ubu_load_config

REDACT=0
APP_VERSION=""
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
    case "$1" in
        --redact) REDACT=1 ;;
        --app-version) APP_VERSION=${2:-}; shift ;;
        *) ubu_die "unknown option: $1" ;;
    esac
    shift
done

R=$ROOTFS
NFAIL=0
NWARN=0

sec() { printf '\n== %s ==\n' "$1"; }
# A blank label continues the previous item.
kv() {
    case "$1" in
        *[!\ ]*) printf '%-24s %s\n' "$1:" "$2" ;;
        *) printf '%-24s %s\n' "" "$2" ;;
    esac
}
ok() { kv "$1" "[ok] $2"; }
warn() { kv "$1" "[warn] $2"; NWARN=$((NWARN + 1)); }
fail() { kv "$1" "[FAIL] $2"; NFAIL=$((NFAIL + 1)); }
# An identifier, or <redacted>.
ident() { if [ "$REDACT" = 1 ]; then printf '<redacted>'; else printf '%s' "$1"; fi; }
prop() { getprop "$1" 2>/dev/null; }

MOUNTED=0
ubu_is_mounted "$R/proc" && MOUNTED=1
CHROOT_PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin
# in_chroot SECS CMD...: inside Ubuntu as root, with a time limit.
in_chroot() {
    _t=$1
    shift
    bb timeout "$_t" chroot "$R" /usr/bin/env -i HOME=/root LANG=C.UTF-8 PATH="$CHROOT_PATH" "$@"
}

# --- Kernel config -------------------------------------------------------------------
CFG=""
if [ -r /proc/config.gz ]; then
    CFG=$(bb zcat /proc/config.gz 2>/dev/null | bb grep -E '^CONFIG_[A-Z0-9_]+=')
fi
# kcfg NAME: y, m or n (unset options are often not listed at all).
kcfg() {
    printf '%s\n' "$CFG" | bb awk -F= -v k="CONFIG_$1" '$1 == k { v = $2 } END { print (v == "" ? "n" : v) }'
}
# shellcheck disable=SC2317,SC2329 # used as a probe command by cap()
in_fs() { bb grep -qw "$1" /proc/filesystems 2>/dev/null; }
CONFIGFS=$(ubu_fs_mount configfs /sys/kernel/config)

# cap LABEL CONFIG_NAME [PROBE...]: from the kernel config when there is one,
# else from the probe command (if any).
cap() {
    _label=$1
    _key=$2
    shift 2
    if [ -n "$CFG" ]; then
        case "$(kcfg "$_key")" in
            y) kv "$_label" "yes" ;;
            m) kv "$_label" "module" ;;
            *) kv "$_label" "no" ;;
        esac
    elif [ $# -gt 0 ]; then
        if "$@" 2>/dev/null; then kv "$_label" "yes (probed)"; else kv "$_label" "not found (probed)"; fi
    else
        kv "$_label" "unknown (no /proc/config.gz)"
    fi
}

# shellcheck disable=SC2317,SC2329 # used as a probe command by cap()
tracing_file() { [ -e "/sys/kernel/tracing/$1" ] || [ -e "/sys/kernel/debug/tracing/$1" ]; }

# A vendor kernel module (.ko) of this ROM, for its vermagic.
vendor_module() {
    for _d in "$(ubu_module_dir 2>/dev/null)" /vendor_dlkm/lib/modules /vendor/lib/modules /system_dlkm/lib/modules /odm/lib/modules; do
        if [ -z "$_d" ] || [ ! -d "$_d" ]; then continue; fi
        for _f in "$_d"/*.ko; do
            [ -f "$_f" ] && { printf '%s' "$_f"; return 0; }
        done
    done
    return 1
}
# The vermagic string from a module's .modinfo (NUL-separated key=value).
ko_vermagic() {
    bb tr '\000' '\n' <"$1" 2>/dev/null | bb awk '/^vermagic=/ { print substr($0, 10); exit }'
}

# --- Hardware inventory ----------------------------------------------------------------
# Device nodes held open by processes: "NODE comm,comm,..." lines, one ls for
# every process (a readlink per fd would fork thousands of times).
OWNERS=""
load_owners() {
    OWNERS=$(bb ls -l /proc/[0-9]*/fd 2>/dev/null | bb awk '
        # App processes (uid >= 10000) are just "app": their names would list
        # the installed apps.
        function name(pid,   f, c, l, u) {
            f = "/proc/" pid "/status"
            while ((getline l <f) > 0) {
                if (l ~ /^Name:/) { c = l; sub(/^Name:[ \t]*/, "", c) }
                if (l ~ /^Uid:/) { split(l, u, /[ \t]+/); break }
            }
            close(f)
            return (u[2] + 0 >= 10000) ? "app" : c
        }
        /^\/proc\/[0-9]+\/fd:$/ { split($0, a, "/"); pid = a[3]; next }
        / -> \/dev\// {
            t = $NF
            if (!((t, pid) in seen)) {
                seen[t, pid] = 1
                if (!(pid in comm)) comm[pid] = name(pid)
                if (!((t, comm[pid]) in named)) { named[t, comm[pid]] = 1; o[t] = o[t] (o[t] == "" ? "" : ",") comm[pid] }
            }
        }
        END { for (t in o) print t, o[t] }')
}
# owners NODE: who holds it open, or "free".
owners() {
    _o=$(printf '%s\n' "$OWNERS" | bb awk -v n="$1" '$1 == n { print $2; exit }')
    printf '%s' "${_o:-free}"
}
# node LABEL PATH...: each existing device node with its owners.
node() {
    _l=$1
    shift
    _any=0
    for _n in "$@"; do
        [ -e "$_n" ] || continue
        kv "$_l" "$_n [$(owners "$_n")]"
        _l="  "
        _any=1
    done
    [ "$_any" = 1 ] || kv "$_l" "none"
}
# names DIR FILE: the FILE of every entry of a sysfs class, comma-separated.
names() {
    _out=""
    for _e in "$1"/*; do
        [ -d "$_e" ] || continue
        _v=$(head -n 1 "$_e/$2" 2>/dev/null)
        _out="$_out${_out:+, }${_e##*/}${_v:+ ($_v)}"
    done
    printf '%s' "${_out:-none}"
}

hardware() {
    sec Hardware
    load_owners
    _soc=$(prop ro.soc.model)
    [ -n "$_soc" ] || _soc=$(bb awk -F': ' '/^Hardware/ { print $2; exit }' /proc/cpuinfo 2>/dev/null)
    kv SoC "${_soc:-unknown} (platform $(prop ro.board.platform))"
    for _p in /sys/devices/system/cpu/cpufreq/policy*; do
        [ -d "$_p" ] || continue
        _mx=$(cat "$_p/cpuinfo_max_freq" 2>/dev/null)
        case "$_mx" in '' | *[!0-9]*) _mx=0 ;; esac
        kv "  CPU ${_p##*/}" "cpus $(cat "$_p/related_cpus" 2>/dev/null), $(cat "$_p/scaling_governor" 2>/dev/null), max $((_mx / 1000)) MHz"
    done
    kv "  CPUs online" "$(cat /sys/devices/system/cpu/online 2>/dev/null)"
    _gpu=$(cat /sys/class/kgsl/kgsl-3d0/gpu_model 2>/dev/null)
    [ -n "$_gpu" ] && kv GPU "$_gpu"
    node "GPU node" /dev/kgsl-3d0 /dev/mali0 /dev/dri/renderD128 /dev/dri/card0
    node framebuffer /dev/graphics/fb0 /dev/fb0
    kv DRM "$(names /sys/class/drm status)"
    _in=$(bb awk '
        /^N: Name=/ { n = substr($0, 10); gsub(/"/, "", n) }
        /^H: Handlers=/ { for (i = 2; i <= NF; i++) if ($i ~ /^event[0-9]+$/) printf "%s%s=%s", (c++ ? ", " : ""), $i, n }
        ' /proc/bus/input/devices 2>/dev/null)
    kv input "${_in:-none}"
    node uinput /dev/uinput
    kv "ALSA cards" "$(bb awk '/^ *[0-9]+ \[/ { sub(/^ */, ""); printf "%s%s", (c++ ? ", " : ""), $0 }' /proc/asound/cards 2>/dev/null)"
    node "ALSA control" /dev/snd/controlC0
    kv V4L2 "$(names /sys/class/video4linux name)"
    kv "IIO sensors" "$(names /sys/bus/iio/devices name)"
    kv "Wi-Fi (sysfs)" "$(names /sys/class/ieee80211 -)"
    kv "Bluetooth HCI" "$(names /sys/class/bluetooth name)"
    _nfc=""
    for _n in /dev/nq-nci /dev/pn5* /dev/pn54* /dev/sec-nfc /dev/nfc* /dev/st21nfc /dev/bcm2079x*; do
        [ -e "$_n" ] && _nfc="$_nfc $_n"
    done
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086 # word splitting intended: a list of nodes
    if [ -n "$_nfc" ]; then node NFC $_nfc; else kv NFC "$(names /sys/class/nfc name)"; fi
    kv "USB gadget UDC" "$(names /sys/class/udc state)"
    _usb=$(cd /sys/bus/usb/devices 2>/dev/null && bb ls -d usb* 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ' | bb sed 's/ $//')
    kv "USB host buses" "${_usb:-none (no host controller active: OTG idle)}"
    kv LEDs "$(cd /sys/class/leds 2>/dev/null && bb ls | tr '\n' ' ' | bb sed 's/ $//')"
    kv "power supplies" "$(names /sys/class/power_supply type)"
    _tz=0
    for _t in /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone*; do [ -d "$_t" ] && _tz=$((_tz + 1)); done
    kv "thermal zones" "$_tz (read-only for UbuRoot)"
    for _c in i2c-:I2C spidev:SPI gpiochip:GPIO ttyHS:"serial (HS)" ttyMSM:"serial (MSM)" ttyUSB:"USB serial" ttyACM:"USB ACM" smd:"modem (SMD, report only)" mhi_:"modem (MHI, report only)"; do
        _pre=${_c%%:*}
        _cnt=0
        for _n in /dev/"$_pre"*; do [ -e "$_n" ] && _cnt=$((_cnt + 1)); done
        [ "$_cnt" -gt 0 ] && kv "${_c#*:}" "$_cnt node(s) (/dev/$_pre*)"
    done
}

report() {
    printf 'UbuRoot doctor\n'
    kv date "$(date +%Y-%m-%d)"
    kv app "${APP_VERSION:-unknown}"
    kv "scripts schema" "$UBU_SCHEMA"
    if [ "$REDACT" = 1 ]; then kv redacted yes; else kv redacted no; fi

    sec Root
    ubu_detect_root_impl
    kv manager "$UBU_ROOT_IMPL ${UBU_ROOT_VERSION}"
    kv busybox "${BB:-none} ($BB_KIND)"
    _rr=$(ubu_root_report)
    _rv() { printf '%s\n' "$_rr" | bb awk -F= -v k="$1" '$1 == k { print substr($0, length(k) + 2); exit }'; }
    if [ "$(_rv full_root)" = yes ]; then
        ok "full root" "all capabilities (cap_last_cap $(_rv cap_last_cap)), no seccomp, no no_new_privs"
    else
        fail "full root" "restricted (a root manager profile?): see below"
    fi
    kv "CapEff" "$(_rv cap_eff)"
    kv "CapBnd" "$(_rv cap_bnd)"
    kv "Seccomp" "$(_rv seccomp)"
    kv "NoNewPrivs" "$(_rv no_new_privs)"
    kv "SELinux context" "$(_rv selinux)"
    if [ "$(readlink /proc/self/ns/mnt)" = "$(readlink /proc/1/ns/mnt)" ]; then
        ok "mount namespace" "global (init)"
    else
        fail "mount namespace" "private: su is not running with --mount-master"
    fi

    sec Android
    kv device "$(prop ro.product.manufacturer) $(prop ro.product.model) ($(prop ro.product.device))"
    kv android "$(prop ro.build.version.release) (SDK $(prop ro.build.version.sdk))"
    _rom=$(prop ro.lineage.version)
    kv rom "${_rom:-$(prop ro.build.display.id)}"
    kv "security patch" "$(prop ro.build.version.security_patch)"
    kv selinux "$(getenforce 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)"
    _cs=$(prop ro.crypto.state)
    _ct=$(prop ro.crypto.type)
    kv encryption "${_cs:-unknown}${_ct:+ ($_ct)}"
    kv "user 0 unlocked" "$(prop sys.user.0.ce_available)"

    sec Kernel
    kv kernel "$(uname -r) $(uname -m)"
    if [ -n "$CFG" ]; then kv config "/proc/config.gz"; else kv config "absent: features below are probed"; fi
    cap "user namespaces" USER_NS test -e /proc/self/ns/user
    cap overlayfs OVERLAY_FS in_fs overlay
    cap fuse FUSE_FS in_fs fuse
    cap "tun/tap" TUN sh -c '[ -e /dev/tun ] || [ -e /dev/net/tun ]'
    cap wireguard WIREGUARD test -d /sys/module/wireguard
    cap binfmt_misc BINFMT_MISC in_fs binfmt_misc
    cap "loop devices" BLK_DEV_LOOP sh -c '[ -e /dev/loop-control ] || [ -e /dev/block/loop0 ]'
    cap kvm KVM test -e /dev/kvm
    cap "bpf syscall" BPF_SYSCALL in_fs bpf
    cap "iptables (xtables)" IP_NF_IPTABLES test -e /proc/net/ip_tables_names
    cap nf_tables NF_TABLES
    cap configfs CONFIGFS_FS in_fs configfs
    cap "USB gadget (configfs)" USB_CONFIGFS test -d "${CONFIGFS:-/nonexistent}/usb_gadget"
    cap "  gadget: NCM" USB_CONFIGFS_NCM
    cap "  gadget: RNDIS" USB_CONFIGFS_RNDIS
    cap "  gadget: ACM serial" USB_CONFIGFS_ACM
    cap "  gadget: mass storage" USB_CONFIGFS_MASS_STORAGE
    cap "  gadget: HID" USB_CONFIGFS_F_HID
    cap "USB serial (OTG)" USB_SERIAL test -d /sys/bus/usb-serial
    cap "USB ACM (OTG)" USB_ACM
    cap cfg80211 CFG80211 test -d /sys/class/ieee80211
    cap mac80211 MAC80211
    cap bluetooth BT test -d /sys/class/bluetooth
    cap CAN CAN test -e /proc/net/can
    cap "loadable modules" MODULES test -e /proc/modules
    kv "modules loaded" "$(bb wc -l </proc/modules 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')"
    _md=$(ubu_module_dir) || _md="none found"
    kv "ROM module dir" "$_md"
    case "$(uname -r)" in
        *-android[0-9]*-*) kv GKI "yes ($(uname -r | bb sed 's/.*-\(android[0-9]*\)-.*/\1/'))" ;;
        *) kv GKI "no (vendor kernel)" ;;
    esac
    cap "module signing" MODULE_SIG
    cap "  signature enforced" MODULE_SIG_FORCE
    _se=$(cat /sys/module/module/parameters/sig_enforce 2>/dev/null)
    [ -n "$_se" ] && kv "  sig_enforce (runtime)" "$_se"
    cap modversions MODVERSIONS
    _ko=$(vendor_module) && kv "vendor vermagic" "$(ko_vermagic "$_ko") (${_ko##*/})"
    if [ -e /sys/kernel/btf/vmlinux ]; then kv BTF "yes (/sys/kernel/btf/vmlinux)"; else cap BTF DEBUG_INFO_BTF; fi
    cap kprobes KPROBES tracing_file kprobe_events
    cap uprobes UPROBES tracing_file uprobe_events
    cap ftrace FTRACE tracing_file trace
    kv namespaces "$(bb ls /proc/self/ns 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ' | bb sed 's/ $//')"
    for _s in kptr_restrict dmesg_restrict perf_event_paranoid unprivileged_bpf_disabled; do
        _v=$(cat "/proc/sys/kernel/$_s" 2>/dev/null) || _v="absent"
        kv "$_s" "$_v"
    done
    _ph=$(prop security.perf_harden)
    [ -n "$_ph" ] && kv "security.perf_harden" "$_ph"
    for _k in $UBU_KERNEL_FS; do
        _t=${_k%%:*}
        _p=${_k#*:}
        _p=$(ubu_fs_mount "$_t" "${_p%%:*}")
        kv "host $_t" "${_p:-not mounted}"
    done
    if [ "$MOUNTED" = 1 ] && [ -x "$R/usr/sbin/iw" ]; then
        _phys=$(in_chroot 10 iw phy 2>/dev/null | bb awk '
            /^Wiphy / { p = $2; m[p] = "no"; inmodes = 0; next }
            /Supported interface modes:/ { inmodes = 1; next }
            inmodes && /^\t\t\* / { if ($2 == "monitor") m[p] = "yes"; next }
            inmodes { inmodes = 0 }
            END { for (p in m) printf "%s monitor=%s ", p, m[p] }' | bb sed 's/ $//')
        kv "Wi-Fi phys" "${_phys:-none}"
    else
        kv "Wi-Fi phys" "unknown (needs Ubuntu mounted and: apt install iw)"
    fi

    hardware

    sec Storage
    _free=$(bb df -Pk /data 2>/dev/null | bb awk 'NR == 2 { print $4 }')
    case "$_free" in
        '' | *[!0-9]*) warn "free on /data" "unknown" ;;
        *)
            if [ "$_free" -lt 1048576 ]; then
                warn "free on /data" "$((_free / 1024)) MB"
            else
                ok "free on /data" "$((_free / 1024)) MB"
            fi
            ;;
    esac
    if ubu_rootfs_installed; then
        ok rootfs "installed at $R"
        _osr="$R/usr/lib/os-release"
        [ -f "$_osr" ] || _osr="$R/etc/os-release"
        kv ubuntu "$(bb awk -F= '$1 == "PRETTY_NAME" { gsub(/"/, "", $2); print $2 }' "$_osr" 2>/dev/null)"
        kv "rootfs arch" "$(ubu_elf_arch "$R/usr/bin/dpkg" || echo unknown)"
        if [ -f "$R/etc/uburoot-setup.done" ]; then ok setup "done"; else warn setup "pending"; fi
        _sch=$(ubu_rootfs_schema)
        if [ "$_sch" -lt "$UBU_SCHEMA" ]; then
            warn "integration schema" "$_sch, scripts have $UBU_SCHEMA (the app migrates it on start)"
        else
            ok "integration schema" "$_sch"
        fi
        kv installed "$(bb awk -F= '$1 == "INSTALLED" { print $2 }' "$R/etc/uburoot-release" 2>/dev/null)"
    else
        warn rootfs "not installed"
    fi

    sec Mounts
    if [ "$MOUNTED" = 1 ]; then
        ok state "mounted ($(ubu_count_mounts_under "$R") mounts under the rootfs)"
        _want="/proc /sys /dev /dev/pts /run"
        [ "$MOUNT_SDCARD" = 1 ] && _want="$_want /sdcard"
        [ "$ANDROID_INTEGRATION" = 1 ] && _want="$_want /data /system"
        _miss=""
        for _m in $_want; do ubu_is_mounted "$R$_m" || _miss="$_miss $_m"; done
        if [ -z "$_miss" ]; then ok expected "all present"; else fail expected "missing:$_miss (Settings > Repair mounts)"; fi
        if ubu_any_shared_under "$R"; then fail propagation "a mount under the rootfs is shared"; else ok propagation "nothing shared"; fi
        _ki=""
        for _k in $UBU_KERNEL_FS; do
            _p=${_k#*:}
            ubu_is_mounted "$R${_p%%:*}" && _ki="$_ki ${_k%%:*}"
        done
        [ -n "$(ubu_mounts_under "$R/usr/lib/modules")" ] && _ki="$_ki modules"
        kv "kernel interfaces" "${_ki:- none (KERNEL_ACCESS=$KERNEL_ACCESS)}"
        kv "chroot processes" "$(ubu_chroot_pids | bb wc -l | tr -d ' ')"
    else
        kv state "not mounted (opening a terminal mounts it)"
    fi

    sec Network
    if [ -x /system/bin/ip ]; then
        /system/bin/ip -o addr show 2>/dev/null | bb awk '$2 != "lo" { print $2, $3, $4 }' |
            while read -r _if _fam _addr; do
                kv "  $_if" "$_fam $(ident "$_addr")"
            done
    fi
    if [ -f "$R/etc/resolv.conf" ]; then
        _dns=""
        for _s in $(bb awk '$1 == "nameserver" { print $2 }' "$R/etc/resolv.conf"); do _dns="$_dns $(ident "$_s")"; done
        kv "DNS servers" "${_dns# }"
    fi
    if [ -n "$(cat /proc/net/ip_tables_names 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
        kv "Android firewall" "legacy xtables"
    else
        kv "Android firewall" "no legacy tables (nftables or none)"
    fi
    if [ "$MOUNTED" = 1 ]; then
        _ipt=$(in_chroot 10 update-alternatives --query iptables 2>/dev/null | bb awk '$1 == "Value:" { print $2 }')
        kv "Ubuntu iptables" "${_ipt:-not installed}"
        _arch=$(ubu_elf_arch "$R/usr/bin/dpkg") || _arch=""
        case "$_arch" in
            amd64 | i386) _mirror=${APT_MIRROR:-http://archive.ubuntu.com/ubuntu/} ;;
            *) _mirror=${APT_MIRROR:-$UBU_PORTS_MIRROR_DEFAULT} ;;
        esac
        _host=${_mirror#http://}
        _host=${_host%%/*}
        _hostname=${_host%%:*}
        _port=${_host#"$_hostname"}
        _port=${_port#:}
        kv "apt mirror" "$_mirror"
        if in_chroot 10 getent hosts "$_hostname" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            ok "DNS lookup" "$_hostname resolves"
            if in_chroot 10 bash -c ": >/dev/tcp/$_hostname/${_port:-80}" 2>/dev/null; then
                ok "mirror reachable" "TCP ${_port:-80} open"
            else
                fail "mirror reachable" "cannot connect to $_hostname:${_port:-80}"
            fi
        else
            fail "DNS lookup" "$_hostname does not resolve (Settings > DNS)"
            kv "mirror reachable" "not checked (no DNS)"
        fi
    else
        kv "DNS / mirror" "not checked (Ubuntu not mounted)"
    fi

    sec Config
    kv MOUNT_SDCARD "$MOUNT_SDCARD"
    kv ANDROID_INTEGRATION "$ANDROID_INTEGRATION"
    kv DEFAULT_USER "$(ident "$DEFAULT_USER")"
    kv APT_MIRROR "${APT_MIRROR:-default}"
    kv KERNEL_ACCESS "$KERNEL_ACCESS"
    kv HARDWARE_ACCESS "$HARDWARE_ACCESS"
    _kc=$("$UBU_SH" "$UBU_BIN_DIR/kernel.sh" status 2>/dev/null | bb awk -F= '$1 == "changes" { print $2 }')
    kv "kernel changes" "${_kc:-0} recorded (reverted by Stop all)"
    kv BOOT_START "$BOOT_START"
    kv INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES "$INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES (now $(cat "$UBU_INOTIFY_FILE" 2>/dev/null))"
    kv "boot module" "$("$UBU_SH" "$UBU_BIN_DIR/boot.sh" --status 2>/dev/null | bb awk -F= '$1 == "module" { print $2 }')"

    printf '\nsummary: %s failed, %s warnings\n' "$NFAIL" "$NWARN"
}

# Identifiers that must not appear in a redacted report, one per line: the
# safety net for anything that slips past ident().
identifiers() {
    for _p in ro.serialno ro.boot.serialno; do prop "$_p"; echo; done
    cat /proc/sys/kernel/hostname 2>/dev/null
    printf '%s\n%s\n' "$UBU_HOSTNAME" "$DEFAULT_USER"
    bb awk -F: '$3 >= 1000 && $3 < 60000 { print $1 }' "$R/etc/passwd" 2>/dev/null
    if [ -x /system/bin/ip ]; then
        /system/bin/ip -o addr show 2>/dev/null | bb awk '{ sub(/\/.*/, "", $4); print $4 }'
    fi
    bb awk '$1 == "nameserver" { print $2 }' "$R/etc/resolv.conf" 2>/dev/null
}

if [ "$REDACT" = 1 ]; then
    _ids=$(identifiers | bb awk 'length($0) > 0 && $0 != "localhost" && $0 != "uburoot" && $0 != "127.0.0.1" && $0 != "::1"' | bb sort -u)
    # Whole words only (a user called "ana" must not turn "analysis" into garbage).
    report | bb awk -v ids="$_ids" '
        BEGIN { n = split(ids, id, "\n") }
        function word(c) { return c ~ /[A-Za-z0-9_:-]/ }
        function red(s, x,   out, i, pre, post) {
            out = ""
            while ((i = index(s, x)) > 0) {
                pre = i > 1 ? substr(s, i - 1, 1) : ""
                post = substr(s, i + length(x), 1)
                if (!word(pre) && !word(post)) out = out substr(s, 1, i - 1) "<redacted>"
                else out = out substr(s, 1, i + length(x) - 1)
                s = substr(s, i + length(x))
            }
            return out s
        }
        { for (k = 1; k <= n; k++) if (id[k] != "") $0 = red($0, id[k]); print }'
else
    report
fi
exit 0

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/enter.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Enter the Ubuntu chroot with a clean environment.
# Usage: enter.sh [user] [command...]
#   user     Ubuntu user (default: DEFAULT_USER from config, else root)
#   command  run it non-interactively instead of a login shell
# Environment (optional, set by the app):
#   UBU_CWD  start directory (path inside the chroot, e.g. /sdcard/Download)
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_require_root
ubu_init
ubu_load_config
"$UBU_SH" "$UBU_BIN_DIR/mount.sh" || ubu_die "mount.sh failed; not entering"

R=$ROOTFS
_user=${DEFAULT_USER:-root}
if [ $# -gt 0 ]; then
    [ -n "$1" ] && _user=$1
    shift
fi
_cmd="$*"

case "$_user" in
    [a-z_]*) ;;
    *) ubu_die "invalid user name: $_user" ;;
esac
case "$_user" in
    *[!a-z0-9_-]*) ubu_die "invalid user name: $_user" ;;
esac
_home=$(bb awk -F: -v u="$_user" '$1 == u { print $6; exit }' "$R/etc/passwd")
[ -n "$_home" ] || ubu_die "user '$_user' does not exist in the rootfs"

# Make sure the login hook exists even on rootfs set up by older versions.
ubu_ensure_profile_hook "$R" || ubu_warn "could not write /etc/profile.d/uburoot.sh"

_cwd=${UBU_CWD:-$_home}
_init=""
[ -z "$_cmd" ] && _init=$INIT_COMMAND
_path=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin

if [ -n "$BB" ] && [ "$BB_KIND" = busybox ]; then
    set -- "$BB" chroot "$R"
else
    set -- chroot "$R"
fi
# Magisk's su hands the root process our tty but setsid()s it without making
# it the controlling terminal (seen on a device: tty_nr=0 while fd 0 is the
# terminal's pts). bash then has no job control, and Ctrl+C goes to the su
# client's process group. util-linux setsid --ctty starts a new session and
# takes the tty over (TIOCSCTTY with force, allowed for root).
if [ -t 0 ] && [ -x "$R/usr/bin/setsid" ]; then
    set -- "$@" /usr/bin/setsid --ctty --wait
fi
set -- "$@" /usr/bin/env -i \
    TERM=xterm-256color COLORTERM=truecolor LANG=C.UTF-8 PATH="$_path" \
    UBU_CWD="$_cwd" UBU_INIT="$_init"

if [ "$_user" = root ]; then
    set -- "$@" HOME="$_home" USER=root LOGNAME=root SHELL=/bin/bash
    # setpriv --init-groups gives root its supplementary groups from
    # /etc/group (aid_inet etc., needed under ANDROID_PARANOID_NETWORK).
    if [ -x "$R/usr/bin/setpriv" ]; then
        set -- "$@" /usr/bin/setpriv --reuid=0 --regid=0 --init-groups --
    fi
    if [ -n "$_cmd" ]; then
        exec "$@" /bin/bash -lc "$_cmd"
    fi
    exec "$@" /bin/bash -l
fi

# Non-root: su - (PAM, initgroups). -w keeps our variables across the env reset.
# Interactive shells must NOT use su -c: it calls setsid() and loses job control.
set -- "$@" /bin/su -w UBU_CWD,UBU_INIT,COLORTERM -l "$_user"
if [ -n "$_cmd" ]; then
    exec "$@" -c "$_cmd"
fi
exec "$@"

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/hangup.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Hang up one terminal session, like closing a terminal window: SIGHUP (then
# SIGCONT) to every process inside the chroot whose controlling terminal is
# that session's pty. Processes started with nohup/setsid survive, as usual.
#
# Usage: hangup.sh PID
#   PID  any process whose stdin is the session's pty (the app passes its own
#        `su` client of that session)
#
# Why not just close the pty: the kernel then signals only the session leader,
# which is util-linux `su -` (enter.sh), and su blocks SIGHUP while it waits
# for its shell. The shell kept running on the device (see CLAUDE.md).
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_require_root
ubu_init

_pid=${1:-}
case "$_pid" in
    '' | *[!0-9]*) ubu_die "usage: hangup.sh PID" ;;
esac

# Device numbers of the pty, through the magic link: right in any mount namespace.
_dev=$(bb stat -L -c '%t %T' "/proc/$_pid/fd/0" 2>/dev/null) || ubu_die "cannot stat stdin of $_pid"
_maj=$(printf '%d' "0x${_dev% *}")
_min=$(printf '%d' "0x${_dev#* }")
# Unix98 pty slaves: majors 136..143 (Documentation/admin-guide/devices.txt).
if [ "$_maj" -lt 136 ] || [ "$_maj" -gt 143 ]; then
    ubu_die "stdin of $_pid is not a pty"
fi
# tty_nr as /proc/PID/stat encodes it (new_encode_dev).
_nr=$(((_maj << 8) | (_min & 255) | ((_min >> 8) << 20)))

_hup=""
for _p in $(ubu_chroot_pids); do
    _s=$(cat "/proc/$_p/stat" 2>/dev/null) || continue
    # Field 7 (tty_nr); comm (field 2) may contain spaces, so cut after its ")".
    _s=${_s##*) }
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086 # split the remaining fields on purpose
    set -- $_s
    [ "${5:-0}" = "$_nr" ] && _hup="$_hup $_p"
done

if [ -z "$_hup" ]; then
    ubu_log "no chroot process on that terminal"
    exit 0
fi
ubu_log "hangup:$_hup"
# shellcheck disable=SC2086 # word splitting of the pid list is intended
kill -HUP $_hup 2>/dev/null
# shellcheck disable=SC2086
kill -CONT $_hup 2>/dev/null
exit 0

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/hardware.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# uburoot-hw: the phone's hardware from Ubuntu, with every change to device state
# recorded in the same change log as uburoot-kernel (Stop all reverts both).
#
# The same file runs on both sides, like kernel.sh:
#   Android: /data/local/uburoot/bin/hardware.sh (the app, adb)
#   Ubuntu:  /usr/local/sbin/uburoot-hw (mount.sh installs it with HARDWARE_ACCESS=1)
#
# Usage: uburoot-hw [-y] COMMAND
#   list                       every class: route, nodes, who uses them
#   info CLASS                 nodes, owners, the route and its limits
#   status                     key=value summary (machine-readable, for the app)
#   grant CLASS USER           USER may use CLASS without root (group membership;
#                              serial/hidraw: also a permission rule for new devices)
#   revoke CLASS USER
#   takeover wifi              stop Android's Wi-Fi so Ubuntu can use wlan0
#   release wifi               give it back (Stop all does it too)
#   cpu                        CPU policies, governors, frequencies, online cores
#   cpu governor POLICY|all GOV
#   cpu min|max POLICY FREQ    a frequency from scaling_available_frequencies
#   cpu online CPU 0|1         never cpu0
#   wakelock on|off            keep the phone from suspending
#   set PATH VALUE             an LED (/sys/class/leds/NAME/brightness|trigger)
#   allow NODE                 a bus node (i2c, spi, gpiochip) for group uburoot-bus
#   apply                      apply the permission rules once (root)
#   watch                      apply them every 2 s (started by mount.sh / grant)
#   -y                         don't ask for confirmation (the app asks)
# Classes: gpu input uinput audio camera usb serial hidraw wifi bluetooth nfc
#          sensors leds power cpu thermal buses modem
#
# Safety floor (no option changes it): thermal trips, charger and battery settings
# and voltages are read-only; PMIC/charger/regulator bus nodes are refused; the
# modem is report-only.
set -u

_self=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
if [ -f "$_self/common.sh" ]; then
    MODE=android
    # shellcheck source=common.sh
    . "$_self/common.sh"
    ubu_detect_busybox
    ubu_load_config
    STATE=$UBU_STATE
    ACCESS=$HARDWARE_ACCESS
    RF=$ROOTFS
else
    MODE=ubuntu
    bb() { "$@"; }
    STATE=${UBU_STATE:-/run/uburoot/state}
    RF=""
fi
die() { printf 'uburoot-hw: %s\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }
say() { printf '%s\n' "$*"; }
_lib="$_self/changes.sh"
[ -f "$_lib" ] || _lib=/usr/local/lib/uburoot/changes.sh
[ -f "$_lib" ] || die "missing $_lib"
# shellcheck source=changes.sh
. "$_lib"
[ "$MODE" = ubuntu ] && ACCESS=$(ubuntu_access HARDWARE_ACCESS)
# Test-only: a fake /dev and /sys tree.
HW=${UBU_HW_ROOT:-}
RULES="$RF/etc/uburoot/hw-rules"
PIDFILE="$RF/run/uburoot/hw-watch.pid"

YES=0
if [ "${1:-}" = -y ]; then
    YES=1
    shift
fi

confirm() {
    [ "$YES" = 1 ] && return 0
    [ -t 0 ] || die "refusing without confirmation (no terminal); pass -y"
    printf '%s [y/N] ' "$1" >&2
    read -r _a || return 1
    case "$_a" in y | Y | yes | YES) return 0 ;; esac
    die "cancelled"
}
need_root() { [ "$(id -u)" = 0 ] || [ "${UBU_ALLOW_NONROOT:-0}" = 1 ] || die "must run as root (sudo uburoot-hw ...)"; }
need_access() {
    need_root
    [ "$ACCESS" = 1 ] && return 0
    if [ "$MODE" = ubuntu ]; then
        die "hardware access is off. Turn on Settings > Hardware access in the app, then Repair mounts."
    fi
    die "hardware access is off (HARDWARE_ACCESS=0 in uburoot.conf)"
}
# Run a command inside Ubuntu (usermod, groupadd...).
in_ubuntu() {
    if [ "$MODE" = android ]; then
        bb chroot "$RF" /usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin "$@"
    else
        "$@"
    fi
}

# --- Classes --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# class_def CLASS sets: NODES (globs), GROUP (name:gid, empty = root only),
# RULE (1 = UbuRoot sets group+mode on new nodes), ROUTE, NOTE.
CLASSES="gpu input uinput audio camera usb serial hidraw wifi bluetooth nfc sensors leds power cpu thermal buses modem"
class_def() {
    NODES=""
    GROUP=""
    RULE=0
    ROUTE=direct
    NOTE=""
    case "$1" in
        gpu)
            NODES="/dev/kgsl-3d0 /dev/dri/* /dev/graphics/fb*"
            GROUP=aid_graphics:1003
            NOTE="Adreno: /dev/kgsl-3d0 (Mesa freedreno/Turnip); others: /dev/dri, /dev/mali0. Used by Linux GUI apps (Phase 4)."
            ;;
        input)
            NODES="/dev/input/event*"
            GROUP=aid_input:1004
            NOTE="evdev: touchscreen, keys, sensors-as-input, USB/Bluetooth keyboards and mice. Android reads the same events."
            ;;
        uinput)
            NODES="/dev/uinput /dev/uhid"
            GROUP=aid_uhid:3011
            NOTE="Virtual keyboards, mice and gamepads. Their events reach Android too: a grantee can type into the phone."
            ;;
        audio)
            NODES="/dev/snd/*"
            GROUP=aid_audio:1005
            ROUTE=bridge
            NOTE="audioserver and the audio HAL hold the sound card; the bridge to Android audio (PulseAudio/PipeWire) and the ALSA takeover are Phase 3b. USB audio cards appear here too."
            ;;
        camera)
            NODES="/dev/video* /dev/media* /dev/v4l-subdev*"
            GROUP=aid_camera:1006
            ROUTE=bridge
            NOTE="Vendor camera drivers need the camera HAL: plain V4L2 tools can't drive them. The Camera2 bridge is Phase 3b."
            ;;
        usb)
            NODES="/dev/bus/usb/*/*"
            GROUP=aid_usb:1018
            NOTE="USB host (OTG): libusb devices (SDR dongles, programmers, NFC readers, adapters). Nodes appear when a device is plugged in."
            ;;
        serial)
            NODES="/dev/ttyACM* /dev/ttyUSB*"
            GROUP=dialout:20
            RULE=1
            NOTE="USB serial adapters and boards (needs the kernel driver: CONFIG_USB_ACM, _FTDI_SIO, _PL2303, _CP210X, _CH341...). Android leaves them root-only; UbuRoot sets group dialout on new ones."
            ;;
        hidraw)
            NODES="/dev/hidraw*"
            GROUP=plugdev:46
            RULE=1
            NOTE="Raw HID (security keys, controllers). Android leaves them root-only; UbuRoot sets group plugdev on new ones."
            ;;
        wifi)
            ROUTE=takeover
            NOTE="Diagnostics (iw) work alongside Android. takeover wifi stops Android's Wi-Fi and Wi-Fi scanning, then wlan0 is Ubuntu's (wpa_supplicant, hostapd). Monitor mode only if the phy lists it."
            ;;
        bluetooth)
            NODES="/dev/ttyHS0"
            ROUTE=none
            NOTE="BlueZ needs an HCI transport in the kernel (CONFIG_BT_HCIUART for the internal chip, CONFIG_BT_HCIBTUSB for USB dongles)."
            ;;
        nfc)
            NODES="/dev/nq-nci /dev/pn54* /dev/pn55* /dev/sec-nfc /dev/st21nfc"
            GROUP=aid_nfc:1027
            ROUTE=none
            NOTE="Phone NFC controllers are vendor chardevs used by the NFC HAL; libnfc/nfcpy can't drive them. USB readers (ACR122U...) work through the usb class."
            ;;
        sensors)
            ROUTE=bridge
            NOTE="Direct only when the sensors are on the kernel's IIO bus (/sys/bus/iio). Otherwise the SensorManager/LocationManager bridge is Phase 3b."
            ;;
        leds)
            ROUTE=direct
            NOTE="/sys/class/leds: read freely; writes with uburoot-hw set (recorded)."
            ;;
        power)
            ROUTE="read"
            NOTE="Battery and chargers: read-only (safety floor). wakelock on/off keeps the phone awake."
            ;;
        cpu)
            ROUTE=direct
            NOTE="cpufreq governors and limits within the hardware table, cores online/offline (not cpu0); recorded. Vendor power daemons may change them back."
            ;;
        thermal)
            ROUTE="read"
            NOTE="Thermal zones and trip points: read-only. Shutdown trips are never changed."
            ;;
        buses)
            NODES="/dev/i2c-* /dev/spidev* /dev/gpiochip*"
            GROUP=uburoot-bus:
            ROUTE="read"
            NOTE="Read-only by default. uburoot-hw allow NODE opens one node to group uburoot-bus (recorded); PMIC, charger and regulator nodes are refused."
            ;;
        modem)
            ROUTE="read"
            NOTE="Report only: data interfaces (rmnet*) can be captured; no AT command or NV path."
            ;;
        *) die "unknown class: $1 (one of: $CLASSES)" ;;
    esac
}

nodes() { # CLASS -> existing nodes, one per line
    class_def "$1"
    for _g in $NODES; do
        for _n in $HW$_g; do
            [ -e "$_n" ] && printf '%s\n' "$_n"
        done
    done
}
# Who holds a node open: "pid/name" list (one ls of every fd).
holders() { # NODE...
    [ $# -gt 0 ] || return 0
    bb ls -l /proc/[0-9]*/fd 2>/dev/null | bb awk -v want=" $* " '
        /^\/proc\// { p = $0; sub("^/proc/", "", p); sub("/fd:$", "", p); next }
        { t = $NF; if (index(want, " " t " ") && !(p in seen)) { seen[p] = 1; print p } }' |
        while read -r _p; do
            printf '%s/%s ' "$_p" "$(cat "/proc/$_p/comm" 2>/dev/null)"
        done
}
group_users() { # NAME -> comma-separated members
    bb awk -F: -v g="$1" '$1 == g { print $4; exit }' "$RF/etc/group" 2>/dev/null
}

# Kernel config check for a class that depends on drivers. 0 = has it or unknown.
kconfig() { # NAME -> y|m|n|? (unknown)
    if [ -r /proc/config.gz ]; then
        _v=$(bb zcat /proc/config.gz 2>/dev/null | bb awk -F= -v k="CONFIG_$1" '$1 == k { print $2 }')
        printf '%s' "${_v:-n}"
    else
        printf '?'
    fi
}
class_state() { # CLASS -> available|missing|taken|unsupported
    class_def "$1"
    case "$1" in
        wifi)
            if rec_get takeover wifi >/dev/null 2>&1; then
                say taken
            elif [ -n "$(ls "$HW/sys/class/ieee80211" 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
                say available
            else
                say missing
            fi
            return
            ;;
        bluetooth)
            case "$(kconfig BT_HCIUART)$(kconfig BT_HCIBTUSB)$(kconfig BT_HCIVHCI)" in
                *y* | *m* | *'?'*) say available ;;
                *) say unsupported ;;
            esac
            return
            ;;
        nfc | modem | power | thermal | leds | cpu) say available; return ;;
        sensors)
            if [ -n "$(ls "$HW/sys/bus/iio/devices" 2>/dev/null)" ]; then say available; else say bridge; fi
            return
            ;;
    esac
    if [ -n "$(nodes "$1" | head -n 1)" ]; then say available; else say missing; fi
}

# --- Groups ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ensure_group() { # NAME:GID -> group name to use
    _name=${1%%:*}
    _gid=${1#*:}
    if bb awk -F: -v g="$_name" '$1 == g { f = 1 } END { exit !f }' "$RF/etc/group"; then
        printf '%s' "$_name"
        return 0
    fi
    if [ -n "$_gid" ]; then
        # The gid may already exist under another name: use that group.
        _other=$(bb awk -F: -v i="$_gid" '$3 == i { print $1; exit }' "$RF/etc/group")
        if [ -n "$_other" ]; then
            printf '%s' "$_other"
            return 0
        fi
        in_ubuntu groupadd -g "$_gid" "$_name" || return 1
    else
        in_ubuntu groupadd -r "$_name" || return 1
    fi
    printf '%s' "$_name"
}
valid_user() { # NAME
    case "$1" in '' | [!a-z_]* | *[!a-z0-9_-]*) die "invalid user name: $1" ;; esac
    bb awk -F: -v u="$1" '$1 == u { f = 1 } END { exit !f }' "$RF/etc/passwd" || die "no user $1 in Ubuntu"
}

grant() { # CLASS USER
    class_def "$1"
    [ -n "$GROUP" ] || die "$1 has no device group to grant (route: $ROUTE)"
    valid_user "$2"
    need_access
    _g=$(ensure_group "$GROUP") || die "cannot create group ${GROUP%%:*}"
    in_ubuntu usermod -aG "$_g" "$2" || die "usermod failed"
    say "$2 is now in group $_g ($1). It applies to new logins (open a new session)."
    if [ "$RULE" = 1 ]; then
        mkdir -p "${RULES%/*}"
        bb grep -qx "$1" "$RULES" 2>/dev/null || printf '%s\n' "$1" >>"$RULES"
        apply
        start_watch
    fi
    case "$1" in
        uinput) say "Note: virtual input devices created by $2 reach Android too." ;;
    esac
}
revoke() { # CLASS USER
    class_def "$1"
    [ -n "$GROUP" ] || die "$1 has no device group"
    valid_user "$2"
    need_root
    _g=$(bb awk -F: -v i="${GROUP#*:}" -v n="${GROUP%%:*}" '$1 == n || (i != "" && $3 == i) { print $1; exit }' "$RF/etc/group")
    [ -n "$_g" ] || { say "$2 had no $1 access"; return 0; }
    in_ubuntu gpasswd -d "$2" "$_g" >/dev/null 2>&1 || say "$2 was not in group $_g"
    if [ "$RULE" = 1 ] && [ -z "$(group_users "$_g")" ] && [ -f "$RULES" ]; then
        bb grep -vx "$1" "$RULES" >"$RULES.tmp"
        mv -f "$RULES.tmp" "$RULES"
    fi
    say "$2 removed from group $_g ($1)"
}

# --- Permission rules (udev-like, no udevd) -----------------------------------------------
# For each enabled rule class, new nodes get the class group and mode 0660; the
# original owner and mode are recorded, so Stop all restores them.
apply() {
    [ -s "$RULES" ] || return 0
    _todo=""
    for _c in $(bb awk 'NF { print $1 }' "$RULES"); do
        case " $CLASSES " in *" $_c "*) ;; *) continue ;; esac
        class_def "$_c"
        [ "$RULE" = 1 ] || continue
        _gid=${GROUP#*:}
        for _n in $(nodes "$_c"); do
            [ -c "$_n" ] || continue
            _st=$(bb stat -c '%g %a' "$_n" 2>/dev/null) || continue
            _cg=${_st%% *}
            _m=${_st#* }
            # Group digit of the mode (660 -> 6): read+write = 6 or 7.
            _gd=$(printf '%s' "$_m" | bb awk '{ print substr($0, length($0) - 1, 1) }')
            if [ "$_cg" = "$_gid" ] && { [ "$_gd" = 6 ] || [ "$_gd" = 7 ]; }; then
                continue
            fi
            _todo="$_todo $_n:$_gid"
        done
    done
    [ -n "$_todo" ] || return 0
    lock_try || return 0
    for _t in $_todo; do
        _n=${_t%:*}
        rec_add node "$_n" "$(bb stat -c '%u:%g:%a' "$_n")"
        if chgrp "${_t##*:}" "$_n" && chmod 0660 "$_n"; then
            say "rule: $_n -> group ${_t##*:}, 0660"
        fi
    done
    # Records of unplugged devices: nothing to restore any more.
    bb awk '$1 == "node" { print $2 }' "$CHANGES" | while read -r _n; do
        [ -e "$_n" ] || rec_del node "$_n"
    done
    unlock
}
watch_running() {
    _p=$(cat "$PIDFILE" 2>/dev/null) || return 1
    [ -n "$_p" ] && kill -0 "$_p" 2>/dev/null
}
start_watch() {
    watch_running && return 0
    [ -s "$RULES" ] || return 0
    if [ "$MODE" = android ]; then
        bb chroot "$RF" /usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin \
            /usr/local/sbin/uburoot-hw watch </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 &
    else
        "$0" watch </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 &
    fi
}
watch_loop() {
    need_access
    mkdir -p "${PIDFILE%/*}"
    printf '%s\n' "$$" >"$PIDFILE"
    while [ -s "$RULES" ]; do
        apply >/dev/null 2>&1
        sleep 2
    done
    rm -f "$PIDFILE"
}

# --- Wi-Fi takeover ---------------------------------------------------------------------
wifi_takeover() {
    need_access
    lock
    rec_get takeover wifi >/dev/null && { say "Wi-Fi is already Ubuntu's"; return 0; }
    case "$(acmd cmd wifi status 2>/dev/null | head -n 1)" in
        *enabled*) _w=1 ;;
        *disabled*) _w=0 ;;
        *) die "cannot read Android's Wi-Fi state (cmd wifi status)" ;;
    esac
    _s=$(acmd settings get global wifi_scan_always_enabled 2>/dev/null)
    [ "$_s" = 1 ] || _s=0
    confirm "Turn off Android's Wi-Fi (and Wi-Fi scanning) so Ubuntu can use wlan0? The phone loses Wi-Fi until release, Stop all or a reboot."
    rec_add takeover wifi "$_w:$_s"
    acmd cmd wifi set-scan-always-available disabled >/dev/null 2>&1
    acmd cmd wifi set-wifi-enabled disabled >/dev/null 2>&1
    # Android's wpa_supplicant leaves once Wi-Fi and scanning are off.
    _i=0
    while [ "$_i" -lt 15 ] && acmd cmd wifi status 2>/dev/null | head -n 1 | bb grep -q enabled; do
        sleep 1
        _i=$((_i + 1))
    done
    say "Wi-Fi is Ubuntu's now (was: wifi=$_w scan=$_s). Next, as root in Ubuntu:"
    say "  ip link set wlan0 up; iw dev wlan0 scan | grep SSID"
    say "  wpa_supplicant -B -i wlan0 -c /etc/wpa_supplicant.conf; dhclient wlan0  (or hostapd for an access point)"
    say "uburoot-hw release wifi (or Stop all) gives it back to Android."
}
wifi_back() {
    need_root
    lock
    _st=$(rec_get takeover wifi) || { say "Wi-Fi is not taken over"; return 0; }
    wifi_release "$_st" || die "could not give Wi-Fi back to Android"
    rec_del takeover wifi
    say "Wi-Fi given back to Android"
}

# --- CPU, power, thermal --------------------------------------------------------------------
CPUF="$HW/sys/devices/system/cpu"
policies() { ls -d "$CPUF"/cpufreq/policy* 2>/dev/null; }
cpu_show() {
    for _p in $(policies); do
        say "${_p##*/}: cpus $(cat "$_p/related_cpus") governor $(cat "$_p/scaling_governor") min $(cat "$_p/scaling_min_freq") max $(cat "$_p/scaling_max_freq") (hw $(cat "$_p/cpuinfo_min_freq")-$(cat "$_p/cpuinfo_max_freq") kHz)"
        say "  governors: $(cat "$_p/scaling_available_governors" 2>/dev/null)"
        say "  frequencies: $(cat "$_p/scaling_available_frequencies" 2>/dev/null)"
    done
    say "online: $(cat "$CPUF/online" 2>/dev/null)"
}
set_file() { # PATH VALUE (recorded)
    _o=$(cat "$1" 2>/dev/null) || die "cannot read $1"
    [ "$_o" = "$2" ] && { say "${1#"$HW"} = $2 (unchanged)"; return 0; }
    wr "$2" "$1" 2>/dev/null || die "the kernel refused ${1#"$HW"} = $2"
    _n=$(cat "$1" 2>/dev/null)
    if _r=$(rec_get file "$1"); then
        [ "$_n" = "$_r" ] && rec_del file "$1"
    else
        rec_add file "$1" "$_o"
    fi
    say "${1#"$HW"} = $_n (was $_o)"
}
policy_dirs() { # POLICY|all
    if [ "$1" = all ]; then
        policies
    else
        case "$1" in policy[0-9]*) ;; *) die "invalid policy: $1 (policy0, policy4... or all)" ;; esac
        [ -d "$CPUF/cpufreq/$1" ] || die "no such policy: $1"
        printf '%s\n' "$CPUF/cpufreq/$1"
    fi
}
cpu_cmd() {
    [ $# -gt 0 ] || { cpu_show; return; }
    need_access
    case "$1" in
        governor)
            [ $# = 3 ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw cpu governor POLICY|all GOVERNOR"
            lock
            for _p in $(policy_dirs "$2"); do
                bb grep -qw -- "$3" "$_p/scaling_available_governors" || die "${_p##*/} has no governor $3"
                set_file "$_p/scaling_governor" "$3"
            done
            ;;
        min | max)
            [ $# = 3 ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw cpu $1 POLICY|all FREQ_KHZ"
            lock
            for _p in $(policy_dirs "$2"); do
                # The table can leave out the boost frequency (S9 policy4: cpuinfo_max_freq
                # 2803200, the table ends at 2649600), so the hardware limits count too.
                if ! bb grep -qw -- "$3" "$_p/scaling_available_frequencies" &&
                    [ "$3" != "$(cat "$_p/cpuinfo_max_freq" 2>/dev/null)" ] &&
                    [ "$3" != "$(cat "$_p/cpuinfo_min_freq" 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
                    die "${_p##*/}: $3 is not one of its frequencies ($(cat "$_p/scaling_available_frequencies"))"
                fi
                set_file "$_p/scaling_$1_freq" "$3"
            done
            ;;
        online)
            [ $# = 3 ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw cpu online CPU 0|1"
            case "$2" in '' | *[!0-9]*) die "invalid CPU: $2" ;; esac
            [ "$2" != 0 ] || die "cpu0 stays online"
            case "$3" in 0 | 1) ;; *) die "online takes 0 or 1" ;; esac
            [ -f "$CPUF/cpu$2/online" ] || die "cpu$2 can't be switched"
            lock
            set_file "$CPUF/cpu$2/online" "$3"
            ;;
        *) die "usage: uburoot-hw cpu [governor|min|max|online ...]" ;;
    esac
}
wakelock() {
    need_access
    lock
    case "${1:-}" in
        on)
            wr uburoot_hw "$POWER/wake_lock" 2>/dev/null || die "cannot take a wake lock"
            rec_add wakelock uburoot_hw -
            say "wake lock held: the phone won't suspend (wakelock off, Stop all or a reboot releases it)"
            ;;
        off)
            wr uburoot_hw "$POWER/wake_unlock" 2>/dev/null
            rec_del wakelock uburoot_hw
            say "wake lock released"
            ;;
        *) die "usage: uburoot-hw wakelock on|off" ;;
    esac
}
power_show() {
    for _s in "$HW"/sys/class/power_supply/*; do
        [ -d "$_s" ] || continue
        _l="${_s##*/}:"
        for _k in type online status capacity health temp voltage_now current_now charge_type; do
            [ -r "$_s/$_k" ] && _l="$_l $_k=$(cat "$_s/$_k" 2>/dev/null)"
        done
        say "$_l"
    done
}
thermal_show() {
    for _z in "$HW"/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone*; do
        [ -d "$_z" ] || continue
        _l="${_z##*/} $(cat "$_z/type" 2>/dev/null) $(cat "$_z/temp" 2>/dev/null)"
        for _t in "$_z"/trip_point_*_type; do
            [ -f "$_t" ] || continue
            [ "$(cat "$_t" 2>/dev/null)" = critical ] && _l="$_l critical=$(cat "${_t%_type}_temp" 2>/dev/null)"
        done
        say "$_l"
    done
}

# --- LEDs and bus nodes -----------------------------------------------------------------
set_led() { # PATH VALUE
    need_access
    case "$1" in
        /sys/class/leds/*/brightness | /sys/class/leds/*/trigger) ;;
        *) die "only /sys/class/leds/NAME/brightness or trigger can be set" ;;
    esac
    case "$1" in *..*) die "invalid path: $1" ;; esac
    case "$2" in *[!A-Za-z0-9_-]* | '') die "invalid value: $2" ;; esac
    lock
    set_file "$HW$1" "$2"
}
# PMIC, charger, regulator and fuel-gauge parts stay out of reach (safety floor).
DENY='pmic|pmi[0-9]|pm[0-9]+|pm8|smb[0-9]|qpnp|charger|regulator|fuel|battery|max777|s2m|bq2[0-9]|sm5|mfc'
bus_label() { # NODE -> what is behind it
    _b=${1##*/}
    case "$_b" in
        gpiochip*) cat "$HW/sys/bus/gpio/devices/$_b/label" "$HW/sys/class/gpio/$_b/label" 2>/dev/null | head -n 1 ;;
        i2c-*) cat "$HW/sys/bus/i2c/devices/${_b#i2c-}"-*/name "$HW/sys/class/i2c-dev/$_b/name" 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ' ;;
        spidev*) cat "$HW/sys/class/spidev/$_b/device/modalias" 2>/dev/null ;;
    esac
}
allow_node() { # NODE
    need_access
    case "$1" in
        /dev/i2c-[0-9]* | /dev/spidev[0-9]* | /dev/gpiochip[0-9]*) ;;
        *) die "allow takes /dev/i2c-N, /dev/spidevN.M or /dev/gpiochipN" ;;
    esac
    [ -c "$HW$1" ] || die "no such device: $1"
    _lab=$(bus_label "$1")
    if printf '%s' "$_lab" | bb grep -qiE "$DENY"; then
        die "$1 ($_lab) belongs to power management (PMIC/charger/regulator): refused"
    fi
    confirm "Give group uburoot-bus read/write access to $1 ($_lab)? A wrong write can hang or damage what is on that bus."
    _g=$(ensure_group uburoot-bus:) || die "cannot create group uburoot-bus"
    _gid=$(bb awk -F: -v g="$_g" '$1 == g { print $3 }' "$RF/etc/group")
    lock
    rec_add node "$HW$1" "$(bb stat -c '%u:%g:%a' "$HW$1")"
    if ! { chgrp "$_gid" "$HW$1" && chmod 0660 "$HW$1"; }; then
        die "cannot change $1"
    fi
    say "$1 ($_lab): group $_g, 0660 until Stop all or a reboot. Grant users with: uburoot-hw grant buses USER"
}

# --- Reports ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
list() {
    printf '%-10s %-9s %-11s %s\n' CLASS ROUTE STATE "NODES / USED BY"
    for _c in $CLASSES; do
        _st=$(class_state "$_c")
        _ns=$(nodes "$_c" | bb wc -l | tr -d ' ')
        class_def "$_c"
        _u=""
        # shellcheck disable=SC2046 # one argument per node
        [ "$_ns" -gt 0 ] && _u=$(holders $(nodes "$_c" | head -n 20))
        printf '%-10s %-9s %-11s %s\n' "$_c" "$ROUTE" "$_st" "$_ns ${_u:+($_u)}"
    done
}
info() { # CLASS
    class_def "$1"
    say "class:  $1"
    say "route:  $ROUTE"
    say "state:  $(class_state "$1")"
    if [ -n "$GROUP" ]; then
        _gid=${GROUP#*:}
        say "group:  ${GROUP%%:*}${_gid:+ (gid $_gid)}; users: $(group_users "${GROUP%%:*}")"
    fi
    say "notes:  $NOTE"
    _list=$(nodes "$1")
    if [ -n "$_list" ]; then
        say "nodes:"
        for _n in $_list; do
            _l="  $(bb stat -c '%n %U:%G %a' "$_n" 2>/dev/null)"
            case "$1" in buses) _l="$_l $(bus_label "$_n")" ;; esac
            say "$_l"
        done
        # shellcheck disable=SC2086 # one argument per node
        _h=$(holders $_list)
        [ -n "$_h" ] && say "used by: $_h"
    fi
    case "$1" in
        cpu) cpu_show ;;
        power) power_show ;;
        thermal) thermal_show ;;
        leds) for _l in "$HW"/sys/class/leds/*; do [ -d "$_l" ] && say "  ${_l##*/} brightness=$(cat "$_l/brightness" 2>/dev/null) max=$(cat "$_l/max_brightness" 2>/dev/null)"; done ;;
        wifi)
            for _p in "$HW"/sys/class/ieee80211/*; do [ -d "$_p" ] && say "  phy ${_p##*/}"; done
            command -v iw >/dev/null 2>&1 && iw list 2>/dev/null | bb awk '/Supported interface modes/ { f = 1; next } f && /^\t\t \* / { printf "  mode %s\n", $2; next } f { f = 0 }'
            ;;
        bluetooth) say "  kernel: BT_HCIUART=$(kconfig BT_HCIUART) BT_HCIBTUSB=$(kconfig BT_HCIBTUSB) BT_HCIVHCI=$(kconfig BT_HCIVHCI)" ;;
        nfc) say "  kernel: NFC=$(kconfig NFC)" ;;
        sensors)
            _l=""
            for _d in "$HW"/sys/bus/iio/devices/*; do [ -e "$_d" ] && _l="$_l ${_d##*/}"; done
            say "  IIO devices:${_l:- none}"
            ;;
        modem)
            _l=""
            for _d in "$HW"/sys/class/net/rmnet* "$HW"/sys/class/net/ccmni* "$HW"/sys/class/net/wwan*; do
                [ -e "$_d" ] && _l="$_l ${_d##*/}"
            done
            say "  data interfaces:${_l:- none}"
            say "  telephony: $(acmd getprop gsm.version.baseband 2>/dev/null)"
            ;;
    esac
}
status() {
    if [ "$ACCESS" = 1 ]; then say "access=on"; else say "access=off"; fi
    for _c in $CLASSES; do
        class_def "$_c"
        _g=${GROUP%%:*}
        printf 'class=%s|%s|%s|%s|%s|%s\n' "$_c" "$ROUTE" "$(class_state "$_c")" "$(nodes "$_c" | bb wc -l | tr -d ' ')" "$_g" "$( [ -n "$_g" ] && group_users "$_g")"
    done
    for _p in $(policies); do
        say "cpu=${_p##*/}|$(cat "$_p/related_cpus")|$(cat "$_p/scaling_governor")|$(cat "$_p/scaling_min_freq")|$(cat "$_p/scaling_max_freq")|$(cat "$_p/cpuinfo_max_freq")"
    done
    _b="$HW/sys/class/power_supply/battery"
    [ -d "$_b" ] && say "battery=$(cat "$_b/capacity" 2>/dev/null)|$(cat "$_b/status" 2>/dev/null)|$(cat "$_b/temp" 2>/dev/null)"
    if rec_get wakelock uburoot_hw >/dev/null 2>&1; then say "wakelock=on"; else say "wakelock=off"; fi
    if watch_running; then say "rules=running"; elif [ -s "$RULES" ]; then say "rules=stopped"; else say "rules=none"; fi
    say "changes=$(rec_count)"
}

[ $# -ge 1 ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw [-y] list|info|status|grant|revoke|takeover|release|cpu|wakelock|set|allow ..."
_cmd=$1
shift
case "$_cmd" in
    list) list ;;
    info)
        [ $# = 1 ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw info CLASS"
        info "$1"
        ;;
    status) status ;;
    grant | revoke)
        [ $# = 2 ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw $_cmd CLASS USER"
        "$_cmd" "$1" "$2"
        ;;
    takeover | release)
        [ "${1:-}" = wifi ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw $_cmd wifi (other classes: see uburoot-hw info CLASS)"
        if [ "$_cmd" = takeover ]; then wifi_takeover; else wifi_back; fi
        ;;
    cpu) cpu_cmd "$@" ;;
    wakelock) wakelock "$@" ;;
    set)
        [ $# = 2 ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw set /sys/class/leds/NAME/brightness VALUE"
        set_led "$1" "$2"
        ;;
    allow)
        [ $# = 1 ] || die "usage: uburoot-hw allow /dev/NODE"
        allow_node "$1"
        ;;
    apply)
        need_access
        apply
        ;;
    watch) watch_loop ;;
    *) die "unknown command: $_cmd" ;;
esac

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/install.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Extract an Ubuntu Base tarball (.tar.gz or .tar.xz) into ROOTFS.
# Usage: install.sh <ubuntu-base-*.tar.gz|.tar.xz> [expected-sha256]
# Checks: root, arm64, >= 3 GB free, SHA256 (when given), empty target, and
# that the rootfs is for this CPU (a local file may not be).
# Extracts into ROOTFS.partial first and renames it on success, so an
# interrupted install never leaves a half-populated ROOTFS.
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_require_root
ubu_init

MIN_FREE_KB=$((3 * 1024 * 1024))

[ $# -ge 1 ] || ubu_die "usage: install.sh <ubuntu-base.tar.gz> [sha256]"
TARBALL=$1
SHA=$(printf '%s' "${2:-}" | tr 'A-F' 'a-f')
case "$SHA" in
    '' | ????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????) ;;
    *) ubu_die "SHA256 must be 64 hex digits" ;;
esac
case "$SHA" in *[!0-9a-f]*) ubu_die "SHA256 must be 64 hex digits" ;; esac
[ -f "$TARBALL" ] || ubu_die "tarball not found: $TARBALL"

_arch=$(uname -m)
if [ "$_arch" != aarch64 ] && [ "${UBU_ALLOW_ANY_ARCH:-0}" != 1 ]; then
    ubu_die "unsupported CPU architecture '$_arch' (arm64/aarch64 required)"
fi

if ubu_rootfs_installed; then
    ubu_die "a rootfs is already installed at $ROOTFS (use reset.sh first)"
fi

mkdir -p "$UBU_BASE" || ubu_die "cannot create $UBU_BASE"
ubu_validate_paths

_free=$(bb df -Pk "$UBU_BASE" | bb awk 'NR == 2 { print $4 }')
case "$_free" in
    '' | *[!0-9]*) ubu_warn "could not determine free space" ;;
    *)
        if [ "$_free" -lt "$MIN_FREE_KB" ]; then
            ubu_die "not enough free space: $((_free / 1024)) MB available, 3072 MB required"
        fi
        ;;
esac

if [ -n "$SHA" ]; then
    ubu_log "verifying SHA256"
    _got=$(bb sha256sum "$TARBALL" | bb cut -d' ' -f1)
    [ "$_got" = "$SHA" ] || ubu_die "SHA256 mismatch: expected $SHA, got $_got"
fi

PARTIAL="$ROOTFS.partial"
# Leftovers from an interrupted install go through the safety rule as well.
ubu_safe_rm "$PARTIAL" || ubu_die "cannot clean $PARTIAL"
if [ -e "$ROOTFS" ]; then
    rmdir "$ROOTFS" 2>/dev/null || ubu_die "$ROOTFS exists and is not empty (use reset.sh)"
fi

# The format comes from the content, not the name: a file picked in the app
# arrives under a cache name.
case "$(bb od -An -tx1 -N6 "$TARBALL" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' \n')" in
    1f8b*) _z=z ;;
    fd377a585a00) _z=J ;;
    *) ubu_die "not a .tar.gz or .tar.xz file: $TARBALL" ;;
esac

mkdir -p "$PARTIAL" || ubu_die "cannot create $PARTIAL"
ubu_log "extracting $(basename "$TARBALL")"
if ! bb tar --numeric-owner -xp${_z}f "$TARBALL" -C "$PARTIAL"; then
    ubu_safe_rm "$PARTIAL"
    ubu_die "extraction failed"
fi
fail_partial() {
    ubu_safe_rm "$PARTIAL"
    ubu_die "$*"
}
if [ ! -x "$PARTIAL/usr/bin/env" ] || [ ! -x "$PARTIAL/usr/bin/dpkg" ]; then
    fail_partial "the archive does not look like an Ubuntu rootfs"
fi
# A local file can be for another CPU: dpkg is a plain ELF binary in Ubuntu Base.
_want=$(ubu_native_arch) || _want=""
_got=$(ubu_elf_arch "$PARTIAL/usr/bin/dpkg") || _got=""
# Always enforced (UBU_ALLOW_ANY_ARCH only lets tests run on a non-arm64 CPU).
if [ -n "$_want" ] && [ -n "$_got" ] && [ "$_got" != "$_want" ]; then
    fail_partial "the rootfs is for $_got, this phone runs $_want"
fi
mv "$PARTIAL" "$ROOTFS" || ubu_die "cannot rename $PARTIAL"
chmod 755 "$ROOTFS"
ubu_log "rootfs installed at $ROOTFS"
exit 0

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/kbuild.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# uburoot-kbuild: build out-of-tree kernel modules for the phone's running kernel,
# inside Ubuntu (installed by mount.sh as /usr/local/sbin/uburoot-kbuild with
# KERNEL_ACCESS=1).
#
# Usage:
#   uburoot-kbuild check          what a module must match to load on this kernel
#   uburoot-kbuild prepare SRC    set up the kernel source tree SRC for the running
#                                 kernel: /proc/config.gz, LOCALVERSION so that
#                                 `make kernelrelease` = `uname -r`, modules_prepare
#   uburoot-kbuild build DIR      build the module in DIR against the prepared tree
# Extra make arguments (e.g. LLVM=1 for kernels built with clang) go in
# UBU_KBUILD_MAKEFLAGS. The source must be the exact kernel the phone runs.
set -u
KCONFIG=${UBU_KCONFIG:-/proc/config.gz}
STATE_DIR=${UBU_KBUILD_STATE:-${XDG_STATE_HOME:-$HOME/.local/state}/uburoot}
SRC_FILE="$STATE_DIR/kbuild-src"
MAKEFLAGS_EXTRA=${UBU_KBUILD_MAKEFLAGS:-}

die() { printf 'uburoot-kbuild: %s\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }
say() { printf '%s\n' "$*"; }

config() { # the running kernel's .config on stdout
    [ -r "$KCONFIG" ] || return 1
    gzip -dc "$KCONFIG" 2>/dev/null
}
opt() { # NAME -> y, m or n
    config | awk -F= -v k="CONFIG_$1" '$1 == k { v = $2 } END { print (v == "" ? "n" : v) }'
}

check() {
    _kr=$(uname -r)
    say "kernel release:     $_kr ($(uname -m))"
    case "$_kr" in
        *-android[0-9]*-*)
            _a=$(printf '%s' "$_kr" | sed 's/.*-\(android[0-9]*\)-.*/\1/')
            _v=$(printf '%s' "$_kr" | cut -d. -f1-2)
            say "kernel type:        GKI ($_a-$_v)"
            say "source:             Android Common Kernel, branch $_a-$_v"
            say "                    (https://android.googlesource.com/kernel/common; the g<hash> in"
            say "                    the release names the exact commit)"
            ;;
        *)
            say "kernel type:        vendor kernel (not GKI)"
            say "source:             the phone vendor's or the ROM's kernel source for this device,"
            say "                    at the exact version above"
            ;;
    esac
    if ! config >/dev/null; then
        say "config:             no $KCONFIG: the kernel's configuration is unknown,"
        say "                    so a matching build can't be prepared"
        return 0
    fi
    say "config:             $KCONFIG"
    _vm="$_kr "
    [ "$(opt SMP)" = y ] && _vm="${_vm}SMP "
    case "$(opt PREEMPT_RT):$(opt PREEMPT)" in
        y:*) _vm="${_vm}preempt_rt " ;;
        *:y) _vm="${_vm}preempt " ;;
    esac
    [ "$(opt MODULE_UNLOAD)" = y ] && _vm="${_vm}mod_unload "
    [ "$(opt MODVERSIONS)" = y ] && _vm="${_vm}modversions "
    case "$(uname -m)" in
        aarch64) _vm="${_vm}aarch64" ;;
        *) _vm="${_vm% }" ;;
    esac
    say "expected vermagic:  $_vm"
    if [ "$(opt MODULES)" != y ]; then
        say "verdict:            this kernel has no loadable module support (CONFIG_MODULES=n)"
        return 0
    fi
    _ok=1
    if [ "$(opt MODULE_SIG_FORCE)" = y ]; then
        say "module signing:     ENFORCED (CONFIG_MODULE_SIG_FORCE=y): only modules signed with the"
        say "                    key this kernel was built with load. Vendors don't publish it, so"
        say "                    out-of-tree modules can't be loaded unless you build the kernel."
        _ok=0
    elif [ "$(opt MODULE_SIG)" = y ]; then
        say "module signing:     on, not enforced: unsigned modules load and taint the kernel"
    else
        say "module signing:     off"
    fi
    if [ "$(opt MODVERSIONS)" = y ]; then
        say "symbol versions:    CONFIG_MODVERSIONS=y: every symbol's CRC must match the running"
        say "                    kernel's build; a mismatch shows as 'disagrees about version of"
        say "                    symbol' in dmesg. Build against the exact source and config."
    fi
    case "$_kr" in
        *-android[0-9]*-*)
            say "GKI symbols:        unsigned modules may only use the symbols GKI exports to vendor"
            say "                    modules; others fail with 'Unknown symbol'"
            ;;
    esac
    if [ "$_ok" = 1 ]; then say "verdict:            modules built for this exact kernel can load"; else say "verdict:            loading out-of-tree modules is blocked by signature enforcement"; fi
}

tools_missing() {
    _miss=""
    command -v make >/dev/null || _miss="$_miss make"
    command -v gcc >/dev/null || command -v clang >/dev/null || _miss="$_miss gcc"
    for _t in bc flex bison; do command -v "$_t" >/dev/null || _miss="$_miss $_t"; done
    printf '%s' "$_miss"
}

prepare() { # SRC
    _src=$(cd -P -- "$1" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P) || die "no such directory: $1"
    grep -q '^VERSION *=' "$_src/Makefile" 2>/dev/null || die "$_src is not a kernel source tree"
    [ "$(id -u)" = 0 ] || [ -w "$_src" ] || die "cannot write to $_src"
    _m=$(tools_missing)
    [ -z "$_m" ] || die "missing tools:$_m (sudo apt install build-essential bc flex bison libssl-dev libelf-dev)"
    config >/dev/null || die "no $KCONFIG: the running kernel's configuration is unknown"
    _base=$(awk -F' *= *' '
        $1 == "VERSION" { v = $2 } $1 == "PATCHLEVEL" { p = $2 }
        $1 == "SUBLEVEL" { s = $2 } $1 == "EXTRAVERSION" { e = $2 }
        END { printf "%s.%s.%s%s", v, p, s, e }' "$_src/Makefile")
    _kr=$(uname -r)
    case "$_kr" in
        "$_base"*) _lv=${_kr#"$_base"} ;;
        *) die "the source is $_base but the phone runs $_kr: use the exact source" ;;
    esac
    for _f in "$_src"/localversion*; do
        [ -e "$_f" ] && say "warning: $_f adds to the version; remove it if kernelrelease doesn't match" >&2
    done
    [ -f "$_src/.config" ] && cp -f "$_src/.config" "$_src/.config.uburoot-old"
    config | sed -e '/^CONFIG_LOCALVERSION=/d' -e '/^CONFIG_LOCALVERSION_AUTO=/d' -e '/^# CONFIG_LOCALVERSION_AUTO is not set/d' >"$_src/.config" ||
        die "cannot write $_src/.config"
    printf 'CONFIG_LOCALVERSION="%s"\n# CONFIG_LOCALVERSION_AUTO is not set\n' "$_lv" >>"$_src/.config"
    say "config from $KCONFIG, LOCALVERSION=\"$_lv\""
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086 # extra make arguments, split on purpose
    make -C "$_src" $MAKEFLAGS_EXTRA olddefconfig modules_prepare || die "make modules_prepare failed"
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
    _got=$(make -s -C "$_src" $MAKEFLAGS_EXTRA kernelrelease 2>/dev/null)
    [ "$_got" = "$_kr" ] || die "kernelrelease is '$_got', the phone runs '$_kr' (vermagic would not match)"
    mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR" && printf '%s\n' "$_src" >"$SRC_FILE"
    say "prepared $_src for $_kr"
    [ -f "$_src/Module.symvers" ] || say "note: no Module.symvers in the tree; with CONFIG_MODVERSIONS the module will carry no CRCs for kernel symbols"
}

build() { # DIR
    _src=$(cat "$SRC_FILE" 2>/dev/null) || die "no prepared tree: run uburoot-kbuild prepare SRC first"
    [ -d "$_src" ] || die "the prepared tree $_src is gone"
    _dir=$(cd -P -- "$1" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P) || die "no such directory: $1"
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
    make -C "$_src" $MAKEFLAGS_EXTRA M="$_dir" modules || die "build failed"
    for _ko in "$_dir"/*.ko; do
        [ -f "$_ko" ] || continue
        say "built $_ko"
        command -v modinfo >/dev/null && say "  vermagic: $(modinfo -F vermagic "$_ko")"
        say "  load it with: sudo uburoot-kmod load $_ko"
    done
}

case "${1:-}" in
    check) check ;;
    prepare)
        [ $# -ge 2 ] || die "usage: uburoot-kbuild prepare SRC"
        prepare "$2"
        ;;
    build)
        [ $# -ge 2 ] || die "usage: uburoot-kbuild build DIR"
        build "$2"
        ;;
    *) die "usage: uburoot-kbuild check | prepare SRC | build DIR" ;;
esac

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/kernel.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# uburoot-kernel: change the phone's kernel state, with every change recorded
# so that Stop all (and "Revert changes") can undo it.
#
# The same file runs on both sides:
#   Android: /data/local/uburoot/bin/kernel.sh (the app, Stop all, adb)
#   Ubuntu:  /usr/local/sbin/uburoot-kernel and uburoot-kmod (mount.sh installs
#            them with KERNEL_ACCESS=1 and binds the change log into the chroot)
#
# Usage: uburoot-kernel [-y] COMMAND
#   status                          key=value summary (machine-readable)
#   sysctl NAME [VALUE]             read a /proc/sys setting, or write it
#   devmode on|off                  kernel addresses readable by root only
#                                   (kptr_restrict=1); off restores the original
#   selinux [enforcing|permissive]  show or switch the whole phone's SELinux mode
#   module list                     loaded modules (* = loaded through UbuRoot)
#   module load FILE.ko|NAME [PARAM=VALUE...]
#   module unload NAME [--force]    --force: a module UbuRoot did not load
#   lastlog                         the previous boot's kernel log (pstore/last_kmsg)
#   revert                          undo every recorded change, newest first
#   uburoot-kmod load|unload|list ...   same as "uburoot-kernel module ..."
#   -y                              don't ask for confirmation (the app asks)
#
# Changes are lost at reboot anyway: the log records the boot id, and a log
# from an earlier boot is discarded. Nothing here persists across reboots.
set -u

_self=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
if [ -f "$_self/common.sh" ]; then
    MODE=android
    # shellcheck source=common.sh
    . "$_self/common.sh"
    ubu_detect_busybox
    ubu_load_config
    STATE=$UBU_STATE
    ACCESS=$KERNEL_ACCESS
else
    MODE=ubuntu
    bb() { "$@"; }
    STATE=${UBU_STATE:-/run/uburoot/state}
fi
die() { printf 'uburoot-kernel: %s\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }
say() { printf '%s\n' "$*"; }
_lib="$_self/changes.sh"
[ -f "$_lib" ] || _lib=/usr/local/lib/uburoot/changes.sh
[ -f "$_lib" ] || die "missing $_lib"
# shellcheck source=changes.sh
. "$_lib"
# Inside Ubuntu, mount.sh writes what was granted to /run/uburoot/access.
[ "$MODE" = ubuntu ] && ACCESS=$(ubuntu_access KERNEL_ACCESS)
# Test-only overrides (never set by the app).
INSMOD=${UBU_INSMOD:-insmod}
MODPROBE=${UBU_MODPROBE:-modprobe}

YES=0
if [ "${1:-}" = -y ]; then
    YES=1
    shift
fi
case "${0##*/}" in
    uburoot-kmod) set -- module "$@" ;;
esac

[ "$(id -u)" = 0 ] || die "must run as root (sudo uburoot-kernel ...)"

confirm() {
    [ "$YES" = 1 ] && return 0
    [ -t 0 ] || die "refusing without confirmation (no terminal); pass -y"
    printf '%s [y/N] ' "$1" >&2
    read -r _a || return 1
    case "$_a" in y | Y | yes | YES) return 0 ;; esac
    die "cancelled"
}

need_access() {
    [ "$ACCESS" = 1 ] && return 0
    if [ "$MODE" = ubuntu ]; then
        die "kernel access is off. Turn on Settings > Kernel access in the app, then Repair mounts."
    fi
    die "kernel access is off (KERNEL_ACCESS=0 in uburoot.conf)"
}

# --- sysctl -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
sysctl_path() { # NAME (kernel.kptr_restrict or kernel/kptr_restrict) -> file
    case "$1" in
        '' | *..* | /* | *[!A-Za-z0-9_./-]*) die "invalid sysctl name: $1" ;;
    esac
    _f="$SYSCTL/$(printf '%s' "$1" | tr '.' '/')"
    [ -f "$_f" ] || die "no such sysctl: $1"
    printf '%s' "$_f"
}
sysctl_key() { printf '%s' "$1" | tr '/' '.'; }
sysctl_set() { # NAME VALUE
    _f=$(sysctl_path "$1") || exit 1
    _k=$(sysctl_key "$1")
    case "$2" in *"
"*) die "the value must be one line" ;; esac
    _orig=$(cat "$_f") || die "cannot read $_k"
    if [ "$_orig" = "$2" ]; then
        say "$_k = $2 (unchanged)"
        return 0
    fi
    wr "$2" "$_f" 2>/dev/null || die "the kernel refused $_k = $2"
    _new=$(cat "$_f")
    if _rec=$(rec_get sysctl "$_k"); then
        [ "$_new" = "$_rec" ] && rec_del sysctl "$_k"
    else
        rec_add sysctl "$_k" "$_orig"
    fi
    say "$_k = $_new (was $_orig)"
}

# --- SELinux ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
selinux_mode() {
    case "$(cat "$ENFORCE" 2>/dev/null)" in
        1) say enforcing ;;
        0) say permissive ;;
        *) say absent ;;
    esac
}
selinux_set() { # 0|1
    [ -f "$ENFORCE" ] || die "SELinux is not available here"
    _cur=$(cat "$ENFORCE")
    [ "$_cur" = "$1" ] && { say "SELinux: $(selinux_mode) (unchanged)"; return 0; }
    if [ "$1" = 0 ]; then
        confirm "Switch SELinux to PERMISSIVE for the whole phone? Every app loses SELinux protection until Stop all, Revert changes or a reboot."
    fi
    wr "$1" "$ENFORCE" 2>/dev/null || die "the kernel refused the SELinux switch"
    [ "$(cat "$ENFORCE")" = "$1" ] || die "SELinux mode did not change"
    if _rec=$(rec_get selinux -); then
        [ "$1" = "$_rec" ] && rec_del selinux -
    else
        rec_add selinux - "$_cur"
    fi
    say "SELinux: $(selinux_mode)"
}

# --- Modules ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
explain_load_error() { # stderr text
    case "$1" in
        *"Key was rejected"* | *"Required key not available"* | *"key"*"rejected"*)
            say "The kernel only loads modules signed with its own key (CONFIG_MODULE_SIG_FORCE). Phone vendors don't publish that key." >&2 ;;
        *"Invalid module format"* | *"Exec format error"*)
            say "The module was built for another kernel (vermagic or symbol versions differ). 'dmesg | tail' says which; uburoot-kbuild check lists what must match." >&2 ;;
        *"Unknown symbol"*)
            say "The module needs symbols this kernel doesn't export to it (on GKI kernels: protected symbols)." >&2 ;;
    esac
}
module_load() { # ARG [PARAMS...]
    need_access
    _arg=$1
    shift
    _before=$(loaded)
    if [ -f "$_arg" ]; then
        confirm "Load $_arg into the phone's kernel?"
        _err=$("$INSMOD" "$_arg" "$@" 2>&1 >/dev/null)
    else
        case "$_arg" in *[!A-Za-z0-9_-]* | '') die "not a file or a module name: $_arg" ;; esac
        [ "$MODE" = ubuntu ] || die "load by name inside Ubuntu (modprobe needs /lib/modules), or pass a .ko file"
        confirm "Load module $_arg (and what it depends on) into the phone's kernel?"
        _err=$("$MODPROBE" "$_arg" "$@" 2>&1 >/dev/null)
    fi
    _rc=$?
    _new=$(loaded | bb awk -v b="$_before" 'BEGIN { n = split(b, a, "\n"); for (i = 1; i <= n; i++) old[a[i]] = 1 } !($1 in old)')
    # /proc/modules lists the newest first: record in load order.
    for _m in $(printf '%s\n' "$_new" | bb awk '{ l[NR] = $0 } END { for (i = NR; i > 0; i--) print l[i] }'); do
        rec_add module "$_m" -
        say "loaded $_m"
    done
    if [ "$_rc" != 0 ]; then
        [ -n "$_err" ] && printf '%s\n' "$_err" >&2
        explain_load_error "$_err"
        exit 1
    fi
    [ -n "$_new" ] || say "nothing new was loaded (already loaded?)"
}
module_unload() { # NAME [--force]
    case "$1" in '' | *[!A-Za-z0-9_-]*) die "invalid module name: $1" ;; esac
    loaded | bb grep -qx "$1" || die "$1 is not loaded"
    if rec_get module "$1" >/dev/null; then
        "$RMMOD" "$1" || die "cannot unload $1"
        rec_del module "$1"
    elif [ "${2:-}" = --force ]; then
        need_access
        confirm "$1 was not loaded by UbuRoot (Android or the ROM loaded it). Unloading it can break the phone, and Revert will NOT load it back. Unload it?"
        "$RMMOD" "$1" || die "cannot unload $1"
    else
        die "$1 was not loaded by UbuRoot; pass --force to unload it anyway"
    fi
    say "unloaded $1"
}

status() {
    say "mode=$MODE"
    if [ "$ACCESS" = 1 ]; then say "access=on"; else say "access=off"; fi
    say "selinux=$(selinux_mode)"
    for _s in kernel/kptr_restrict kernel/dmesg_restrict kernel/perf_event_paranoid kernel/unprivileged_bpf_disabled; do
        _v=$(cat "$SYSCTL/$_s" 2>/dev/null) || continue
        say "$(sysctl_key "$_s")=$_v"
    done
    if rec_get sysctl kernel.kptr_restrict >/dev/null; then say "devmode=on"; else say "devmode=off"; fi
    say "modules_loaded=$(loaded | bb wc -l | tr -d ' ')"
    while read -r _t _n _v; do
        [ -n "$_t" ] && say "change=$_t $_n $_v"
    done <"$CHANGES"
    say "changes=$(rec_count)"
}

[ $# -ge 1 ] || die "usage: uburoot-kernel [-y] status|sysctl|devmode|selinux|module|lastlog|revert ..."
_cmd=$1
shift
case "$_cmd" in
    status)
        lock
        status
        ;;
    sysctl)
        [ $# -ge 1 ] || die "usage: uburoot-kernel sysctl NAME [VALUE]"
        if [ $# = 1 ]; then
            cat "$(sysctl_path "$1")"
        else
            need_access
            lock
            sysctl_set "$1" "$2"
        fi
        ;;
    devmode)
        need_access
        lock
        case "${1:-}" in
            on)
                _cur=$(cat "$SYSCTL/kernel/kptr_restrict" 2>/dev/null) || die "no kptr_restrict"
                if [ "$_cur" -gt 1 ] 2>/dev/null; then
                    sysctl_set kernel.kptr_restrict 1
                else
                    say "kernel.kptr_restrict = $_cur (already readable by root)"
                fi
                say "Root (and only root) can now read kernel addresses (/proc/kallsyms). dmesg and perf already work for root: dmesg_restrict and perf_event_paranoid only limit apps."
                ;;
            off)
                if _o=$(rec_get sysctl kernel.kptr_restrict); then
                    sysctl_set kernel.kptr_restrict "$_o"
                else
                    say "developer mode is off"
                fi
                ;;
            *) die "usage: uburoot-kernel devmode on|off" ;;
        esac
        ;;
    selinux)
        case "${1:-}" in
            '') selinux_mode ;;
            enforcing) lock && selinux_set 1 ;;
            permissive) need_access && lock && selinux_set 0 ;;
            *) die "usage: uburoot-kernel selinux [enforcing|permissive]" ;;
        esac
        ;;
    module)
        _sub=${1:-list}
        [ $# -gt 0 ] && shift
        case "$_sub" in
            list)
                lock
                for _m in $(loaded); do
                    if rec_get module "$_m" >/dev/null; then say "* $_m"; else say "  $_m"; fi
                done
                ;;
            load)
                [ $# -ge 1 ] || die "usage: uburoot-kmod load FILE.ko|NAME [PARAM=VALUE...]"
                lock
                module_load "$@"
                ;;
            unload)
                [ $# -ge 1 ] || die "usage: uburoot-kmod unload NAME [--force]"
                lock
                module_unload "$@"
                ;;
            *) die "usage: uburoot-kmod list|load|unload ..." ;;
        esac
        ;;
    lastlog)
        for _f in /sys/fs/pstore/console-ramoops-0 /sys/fs/pstore/console-ramoops /proc/last_kmsg; do
            if [ -s "$_f" ]; then
                cat "$_f"
                exit 0
            fi
        done
        die "no log of the previous boot (no pstore console or /proc/last_kmsg)"
        ;;
    revert)
        lock
        revert
        ;;
    *) die "unknown command: $_cmd" ;;
esac

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/mount.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Mount everything the chroot needs. Idempotent: every mount is skipped when
# its target already appears in /proc/self/mounts.
# Must run in the global mount namespace (su --mount-master) so that all
# sessions see the same mounts.
# Usage: mount.sh
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_require_root
ubu_init
ubu_load_config
ubu_rootfs_installed || ubu_die "no Ubuntu rootfs at $ROOTFS (run install.sh first)"

R=$ROOTFS

# 0) Self-bind the rootfs and make it private. /data is a shared mount on
#    Android, so without this every mount below would propagate to peers.
#    This is also the probe that our mount binary supports propagation flags;
#    nothing below (rbind!) is safe without them.
if ! ubu_is_mounted "$R"; then
    bb mount -o bind "$R" "$R" || ubu_die "self-bind of $R failed"
fi
if ! ubu_set_propagation private "$R"; then
    bb umount "$R" 2>/dev/null
    ubu_die "cannot set mount propagation (busybox/toybox without --make-private?); refusing to mount"
fi
# /data is nosuid on Android and the self-bind inherits it, which breaks sudo,
# su and ping inside the chroot. Remount this bind without it; a remount
# replaces all per-mount flags, so nodev is passed again explicitly.
bb mount -o remount,bind,nodev "$R" "$R" 2>/dev/null ||
    ubu_warn "could not clear nosuid on $R (sudo will not work)"

# 1) Kernel filesystems and devices
ubu_mount_fs proc proc "$R/proc" || ubu_die "mount proc failed"
ubu_mount_fs sysfs sysfs "$R/sys" || ubu_die "mount sysfs failed"
ubu_bind bind /dev "$R/dev" || ubu_die "bind /dev failed"
ubu_bind bind /dev/pts "$R/dev/pts" || ubu_die "bind /dev/pts failed"
# $R/dev is the real Android /dev: dev/shm and dev/fd get created there.
ubu_mount_fs tmpfs tmpfs "$R/dev/shm" "mode=1777,nosuid,nodev" || ubu_warn "mount /dev/shm failed"
[ -e /dev/fd ] || ln -s /proc/self/fd /dev/fd 2>/dev/null
for _i in 0:stdin 1:stdout 2:stderr; do
```
