```sh
    [ -e "/dev/${_i#*:}" ] || ln -s "/proc/self/fd/${_i%%:*}" "/dev/${_i#*:}" 2>/dev/null
done
if ubu_mount_fs tmpfs tmpfs "$R/run" "mode=0755,nosuid,nodev"; then
    mkdir -p "$R/run/lock" && chmod 1777 "$R/run/lock"
else
    ubu_warn "mount /run failed"
fi

# 1b) Kernel access [KERNEL_ACCESS]: the kernel's own interfaces inside Ubuntu,
#     at the paths a normal Linux has them. Root in Ubuntu then controls the
#     phone's kernel; every change made through uburoot-kernel is recorded in
#     $UBU_STATE and reverted by Stop all.
if [ "$KERNEL_ACCESS" = 1 ]; then
    for _k in $UBU_KERNEL_FS; do
        _t=${_k%%:*}
        _rest=${_k#*:}
        _p=${_rest%%:*}
        _how=${_rest#*:}
        _dst="$R$_p"
        # The kernel creates these directories in sysfs/proc only when it
        # supports the filesystem; never mkdir there.
        [ -d "$_dst" ] || continue
        ubu_is_mounted "$_dst" && continue
        _src=$(ubu_fs_mount "$_t" "$_p")
        if [ -n "$_src" ]; then
            ubu_bind bind "$_src" "$_dst" || ubu_warn "bind $_src ($_t) -> $_p failed"
        elif [ "$_how" = new ] && bb grep -qw "$_t" /proc/filesystems 2>/dev/null; then
            # Under the private rootfs a new mount is private too.
            ubu_mount_fs "$_t" "$_t" "$_dst" || ubu_warn "mount $_t at $_p failed"
        fi
    done
    # Android's cgroup v1 controllers (cpuset, cpuctl, memcg...) live under
    # /dev; $R/dev is a non-recursive bind, so bind each at the same path.
    for _c in $(bb awk -v r="$R" '$3 == "cgroup" && index($2, "/dev/") == 1 && index($2, r "/") != 1 { print $2 }' "$UBU_MOUNTS_FILE"); do
        if [ -d "$R$_c" ] && ! ubu_is_mounted "$R$_c"; then
            ubu_bind bind "$_c" "$R$_c" || ubu_warn "bind cgroup $_c failed"
        fi
    done

    # The ROM's kernel modules, read-only, under /lib/modules/$(uname -r)/uburoot/;
    # depmod writes modules.dep into the rootfs, never into vendor.
    _kv=$(uname -r)
    _mods=""
    for _s in $UBU_MODULE_SOURCES; do
        [ -d "$_s/$_kv" ] && _s="$_s/$_kv"
        [ -d "$_s" ] || continue
        [ -n "$(bb find "$_s" -name '*.ko*' 2>/dev/null | head -n 1)" ] || continue
        _name=$(printf '%s' "${_s#/}" | tr '/' '_')
        _dst="$R/usr/lib/modules/$_kv/uburoot/$_name"
        if ! ubu_is_mounted "$_dst"; then
            if ubu_bind bind "$_s" "$_dst"; then
                bb mount -o remount,bind,ro "$_dst" 2>/dev/null || ubu_warn "could not make $_dst read-only"
            else
                ubu_warn "bind $_s -> modules failed"
                continue
            fi
        fi
        _mods="$_mods $_s"
    done
    _md="$R/usr/lib/modules/$_kv"
    if [ -n "$_mods" ]; then
        if [ ! -e "$R/usr/sbin/depmod" ] && [ ! -e "$R/sbin/depmod" ]; then
            ubu_log "kernel modules found ($_mods ), but Ubuntu has no depmod: apt install kmod"
        elif [ ! -f "$_md/modules.dep" ] || [ "$(cat "$_md/.uburoot-sources" 2>/dev/null)" != "$_mods" ]; then
            if bb chroot "$R" /usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin depmod -a "$_kv" 2>/dev/null; then
                printf '%s' "$_mods" >"$_md/.uburoot-sources"
            else
                ubu_warn "depmod failed for $_kv"
            fi
        fi
    fi

elif ubu_is_mounted "$R/sys/kernel/config" || ubu_is_mounted "$R/sys/kernel/tracing"; then
    ubu_log "KERNEL_ACCESS=0 but the kernel interfaces are still mounted (use 'Reparar mounts')"
fi

# 1c) The change log and the Ubuntu-side tools [KERNEL_ACCESS / HARDWARE_ACCESS].
#     uburoot-kernel and uburoot-hw record every change in $UBU_STATE, shared
#     with the host side, so Stop all (kernel.sh -y revert) undoes them.
ubu_install_tool() { # SOURCE DEST MODE
    # Ubuntu's own /bin/sh: /system/bin/sh is there only with ANDROID_INTEGRATION.
    bb sed '1s|^#!/system/bin/sh$|#!/bin/sh|' "$UBU_BIN_DIR/$1" >"$2.tmp" &&
        chown 0:0 "$2.tmp" && chmod "$3" "$2.tmp" && mv -f "$2.tmp" "$2"
}
mkdir -p "$R/run/uburoot" && chmod 700 "$R/run/uburoot"
printf 'KERNEL_ACCESS=%s\nHARDWARE_ACCESS=%s\n' "$KERNEL_ACCESS" "$HARDWARE_ACCESS" >"$R/run/uburoot/access"
if [ "$KERNEL_ACCESS" = 1 ] || [ "$HARDWARE_ACCESS" = 1 ]; then
    mkdir -p "$UBU_STATE" && chmod 700 "$UBU_STATE"
    if ! ubu_is_mounted "$R/run/uburoot/state"; then
        mkdir -p "$R/run/uburoot/state"
        ubu_bind bind "$UBU_STATE" "$R/run/uburoot/state" || ubu_warn "bind $UBU_STATE failed"
    fi
    if mkdir -p "$R/usr/local/sbin" "$R/usr/local/lib/uburoot"; then
        ubu_install_tool changes.sh "$R/usr/local/lib/uburoot/changes.sh" 644 || ubu_warn "cannot install changes.sh"
        if [ "$KERNEL_ACCESS" = 1 ]; then
            ubu_install_tool kernel.sh "$R/usr/local/sbin/uburoot-kernel" 755 || ubu_warn "cannot install uburoot-kernel"
            ubu_install_tool kbuild.sh "$R/usr/local/sbin/uburoot-kbuild" 755 || ubu_warn "cannot install uburoot-kbuild"
            ln -sf uburoot-kernel "$R/usr/local/sbin/uburoot-kmod"
        fi
        if [ "$HARDWARE_ACCESS" = 1 ]; then
            ubu_install_tool hardware.sh "$R/usr/local/sbin/uburoot-hw" 755 || ubu_warn "cannot install uburoot-hw"
        fi
    fi
    # Permission rules for hotplugged devices (serial, hidraw) granted earlier.
    if [ "$HARDWARE_ACCESS" = 1 ] && [ -s "$R/etc/uburoot/hw-rules" ]; then
        "$UBU_SH" "$UBU_BIN_DIR/hardware.sh" apply >/dev/null 2>&1
        bb chroot "$R" /usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin \
            /usr/local/sbin/uburoot-hw watch </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 &
    fi
fi

# The kernel hostname is shared with Android and must not be changed; make it
# resolvable inside the chroot (sudo warns on every call otherwise).
_kh=$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/hostname 2>/dev/null) || _kh=""
if [ -n "$_kh" ] && [ -f "$R/etc/hosts" ] &&
    ! bb awk -v h="$_kh" '$1 !~ /^#/ { for (i = 2; i <= NF; i++) if ($i == h) f = 1 } END { exit !f }' "$R/etc/hosts"; then
    printf '127.0.1.1   %s\n' "$_kh" >>"$R/etc/hosts"
fi

# 2) Shared storage [MOUNT_SDCARD]
if [ "$MOUNT_SDCARD" = 1 ]; then
    if _src=$(ubu_sdcard_source); then
        ubu_bind bind "$_src" "$R/sdcard" || ubu_warn "bind $_src -> /sdcard failed"
    else
        ubu_warn "no usable shared storage source found; /sdcard not mounted"
    fi
elif ubu_is_mounted "$R/sdcard"; then
    ubu_log "MOUNT_SDCARD=0 but /sdcard is still mounted (use 'Reparar mounts')"
fi

# 3) Android integration [ANDROID_INTEGRATION]: same paths inside the chroot
#    so bionic binaries (linker64, /system/bin/*) work.
if [ "$ANDROID_INTEGRATION" = 1 ]; then
    for _p in $UBU_ANDROID_PARTS; do
        if [ -L "$_p" ]; then
            # e.g. /product -> /system/product: mirror the symlink itself.
            if [ ! -e "$R$_p" ] && [ ! -L "$R$_p" ]; then
                ln -s "$(readlink "$_p")" "$R$_p" || ubu_warn "symlink $_p failed"
            fi
        elif [ -d "$_p" ]; then
            ubu_bind rbind "$_p" "$R$_p" || ubu_warn "rbind $_p failed"
        fi
    done
    # Non-recursive: only /data itself, never the rootfs mounts below it.
    ubu_bind bind /data "$R/data" || ubu_warn "bind /data failed"

    if [ -x /system/bin/sh ]; then
        "$UBU_SH" "$UBU_BIN_DIR/android-env" --if-needed || ubu_warn "android-env failed"
    fi
else
    for _p in $UBU_ANDROID_PARTS /data; do
        if ubu_is_mounted "$R$_p"; then
            ubu_log "ANDROID_INTEGRATION=0 but $_p is still mounted (use 'Reparar mounts')"
            break
        fi
    done
fi

# 4) inotify watches [INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES] for IDEs, file watchers and
#    webpack (Android's default, 8192, is far too low for VS Code). This is a
#    global kernel setting shared with Android, so it is only ever raised,
#    never lowered; 0 leaves it alone. Reset by a reboot.
case "$INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES" in
    '' | *[!0-9]*) ubu_warn "invalid INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES: $INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES" ;;
    0) ;;
    *)
        _cur=$(cat "$UBU_INOTIFY_FILE" 2>/dev/null) || _cur=""
        case "$_cur" in
            '' | *[!0-9]*) ubu_warn "cannot read $UBU_INOTIFY_FILE" ;;
            *)
                if [ "$_cur" -lt "$INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES" ]; then
                    if echo "$INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES" >"$UBU_INOTIFY_FILE" 2>/dev/null; then
                        ubu_log "inotify max_user_watches: $_cur -> $INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES"
                    else
                        ubu_warn "cannot raise inotify max_user_watches ($_cur)"
                    fi
                fi
                ;;
        esac
        ;;
esac

exit 0

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/reset.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Remove the Ubuntu rootfs. This is the ONLY removal path, and it enforces
# the critical rule:
#   umount.sh --kill -> verify /proc/self/mounts has nothing under ROOTFS
#   -> abort on any leftover -> only then rm -rf (inside ubu_safe_rm).
# Usage: reset.sh --yes
# Exit codes: 0 removed, 1 usage/umount error, 3 refused by the safety rule.
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_require_root
ubu_init

[ "${1:-}" = --yes ] || ubu_die "refusing without --yes (this deletes the whole Ubuntu system)"

if [ ! -e "$ROOTFS" ] && [ ! -e "$ROOTFS.partial" ]; then
    ubu_log "nothing to remove"
    exit 0
fi

if ! "$UBU_SH" "$UBU_BIN_DIR/umount.sh" --kill; then
    ubu_log "ABORTED: umount.sh failed; NOTHING was deleted"
    exit 3
fi

_rc=0
ubu_safe_rm "$ROOTFS" || _rc=$?
if [ "$_rc" -ne 0 ]; then
    ubu_log "ABORTED: rootfs NOT deleted (safety rule)"
    exit 3
fi
ubu_safe_rm "$ROOTFS.partial" || exit 3
ubu_log "Ubuntu removed"
exit 0

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/rootinfo.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Print machine-readable facts about the root environment (key=value lines).
# Used by the app's preflight checks; also handy via adb.
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_detect_busybox
ubu_detect_root_impl
printf 'uid=%s\n' "$(id -u)"
printf 'impl=%s\n' "$UBU_ROOT_IMPL"
printf 'version=%s\n' "$UBU_ROOT_VERSION"
printf 'mount_master_flag=%s\n' "$UBU_MOUNT_MASTER_FLAG"
printf 'busybox=%s\n' "${BB:-none}"
printf 'busybox_kind=%s\n' "$BB_KIND"
printf 'arch=%s\n' "$(uname -m)"
printf 'mnt_ns=%s\n' "$(readlink /proc/self/ns/mnt 2>/dev/null)"
printf 'init_mnt_ns=%s\n' "$(readlink /proc/1/ns/mnt 2>/dev/null)"
if [ -d "$UBU_BASE" ]; then
    printf 'free_kb=%s\n' "$(bb df -Pk "$UBU_BASE" | bb awk 'NR == 2 { print $4 }')"
fi
if ubu_rootfs_installed; then printf 'rootfs=installed\n'; else printf 'rootfs=absent\n'; fi
if [ -f "$ROOTFS/etc/uburoot-setup.done" ]; then printf 'setup=done\n'; else printf 'setup=pending\n'; fi
printf 'schema=%s\n' "$(ubu_rootfs_schema)"
printf 'schema_current=%s\n' "$UBU_SCHEMA"
ubu_root_report

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/setup.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Post-install configuration of the Ubuntu rootfs. Safe to re-run.
# Usage: setup.sh [options]
#   --user NAME        create this user (with sudo)
#   --uid N            UID/GID for the user (default: first free from 9000; must be < 10000, see CLAUDE.md)
#   --nopasswd         passwordless sudo for the user
#   --password-stdin   read the user's password from the first line of stdin
#   --dns "A B"        DNS servers (default: DNS_SERVERS from config)
#   --hostname NAME    hostname (default: UBU_HOSTNAME from config)
#   --no-apt           skip apt-get update / package installation (offline)
#   --dns-only         only rewrite /etc/resolv.conf (the app's DNS setting), then exit
#   --sources-only     only rewrite the APT sources (the app's mirror setting), then exit
#   --migrate          only bring the integration files of an installed rootfs up to
#                      this version (numbered migrations, see migrate_N below), then exit
#
# /etc/uburoot-release records SCHEMA (integration version, UBU_SCHEMA in
# common.sh), INSTALLED (first setup) and UBUNTU (VERSION_ID codename).
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_require_root
ubu_init
ubu_load_config
ubu_rootfs_installed || ubu_die "no Ubuntu rootfs at $ROOTFS (run install.sh first)"

R=$ROOTFS
NEW_USER=""
NEW_UID=""
NOPASSWD=0
PASSWORD_STDIN=0
DNS=$DNS_SERVERS
HOST=$UBU_HOSTNAME
DO_APT=1
DNS_ONLY=0
SOURCES_ONLY=0
MIGRATE=0

while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
    case "$1" in
        --user) NEW_USER=${2:-}; shift ;;
        --uid) NEW_UID=${2:-}; shift ;;
        --nopasswd) NOPASSWD=1 ;;
        --password-stdin) PASSWORD_STDIN=1 ;;
        --dns) DNS=${2:-}; shift ;;
        --hostname) HOST=${2:-}; shift ;;
        --no-apt) DO_APT=0 ;;
        --dns-only) DNS_ONLY=1 ;;
        --sources-only) SOURCES_ONLY=1 ;;
        --migrate) MIGRATE=1 ;;
        *) ubu_die "unknown option: $1" ;;
    esac
    shift
done

if [ -n "$NEW_USER" ]; then
    case "$NEW_USER" in
        root) ubu_die "--user must not be root" ;;
        [a-z_]*) ;;
        *) ubu_die "invalid user name: $NEW_USER" ;;
    esac
    case "$NEW_USER" in
        *[!a-z0-9_-]*) ubu_die "invalid user name: $NEW_USER" ;;
    esac
    if [ -n "$NEW_UID" ]; then
        case "$NEW_UID" in
            *[!0-9]*) ubu_die "invalid uid: $NEW_UID" ;;
        esac
        [ "$NEW_UID" -ge 1000 ] || ubu_die "uid must be >= 1000"
    fi
fi
case "$HOST" in
    '' | *[!a-zA-Z0-9-]*) ubu_die "invalid hostname: $HOST" ;;
esac
[ -n "$DNS" ] || ubu_die "no DNS servers"
for _s in $DNS; do
    case "$_s" in
        *[!0-9a-fA-F.:]*) ubu_die "invalid DNS server: $_s" ;;
    esac
done

USER_PW=""
if [ "$PASSWORD_STDIN" = 1 ]; then
    IFS= read -r USER_PW || [ -n "$USER_PW" ] || ubu_die "no password on stdin"
fi

write_resolv_conf() {
    rm -f "$R/etc/resolv.conf"
    {
        printf '# Written by UbuRoot setup.sh (DNS configurable in the app)\n'
        for _s in $DNS; do printf 'nameserver %s\n' "$_s"; done
    } >"$R/etc/resolv.conf" || ubu_die "cannot write resolv.conf"
}

if [ "$DNS_ONLY" = 1 ]; then
    [ -d "$R/etc" ] || ubu_die "no rootfs at $R"
    write_resolv_conf
    ubu_log "resolv.conf: $DNS"
    exit 0
fi

# --- APT sources -----------------------------------------------------------------
# The architecture comes from dpkg's ELF header: no chroot (and no mounts) needed.
write_sources() {
    _osr="$R/usr/lib/os-release"
    [ -f "$_osr" ] || _osr="$R/etc/os-release"
    CODENAME=$(bb awk -F= '$1 == "VERSION_CODENAME" { gsub(/"/, "", $2); print $2 }' "$_osr")
    [ -n "$CODENAME" ] || ubu_die "cannot determine Ubuntu codename"
    ARCH=$(ubu_elf_arch "$R/usr/bin/dpkg") || ubu_die "cannot determine the rootfs architecture"
    # APT_MIRROR serves the release suites; security updates always come from
    # Canonical (security.ubuntu.com, or the ports archive for non-x86).
    case "$ARCH" in
        amd64 | i386)
            _def=http://archive.ubuntu.com/ubuntu/
            SEC_URI=http://security.ubuntu.com/ubuntu/
            ;;
        *)
            _def=$UBU_PORTS_MIRROR_DEFAULT
            SEC_URI=$UBU_PORTS_MIRROR_DEFAULT
            ;;
    esac
    MAIN_URI=${APT_MIRROR:-$_def}
    ubu_valid_mirror "$MAIN_URI" || ubu_die "invalid APT_MIRROR: $MAIN_URI (http://host/path/)"
    ubu_log "apt sources: $CODENAME/$ARCH -> $MAIN_URI"
    mkdir -p "$R/etc/apt/sources.list.d"
    cat >"$R/etc/apt/sources.list.d/ubuntu.sources" <<EOF
# Written by UbuRoot setup.sh
Types: deb
URIs: $MAIN_URI
Suites: $CODENAME $CODENAME-updates $CODENAME-backports
Components: main restricted universe multiverse
Signed-By: /usr/share/keyrings/ubuntu-archive-keyring.gpg

Types: deb
URIs: $SEC_URI
Suites: $CODENAME-security
Components: main restricted universe multiverse
Signed-By: /usr/share/keyrings/ubuntu-archive-keyring.gpg
EOF
    printf '# UbuRoot: sources are in /etc/apt/sources.list.d/ubuntu.sources\n' >"$R/etc/apt/sources.list"
}

if [ "$SOURCES_ONLY" = 1 ]; then
    write_sources
    exit 0
fi

if [ "$MIGRATE" = 1 ]; then
    [ -f "$R/etc/uburoot-setup.done" ] || ubu_die "setup has not run yet: nothing to migrate"
    FROM=$(ubu_rootfs_schema)
    if [ "$FROM" -ge "$UBU_SCHEMA" ]; then
        [ "$FROM" -eq "$UBU_SCHEMA" ] ||
            ubu_warn "rootfs schema $FROM is newer than these scripts ($UBU_SCHEMA); leaving it alone"
        ubu_log "integration files are up to date (schema $FROM)"
        exit 0
    fi
fi

"$UBU_SH" "$UBU_BIN_DIR/mount.sh" || ubu_die "mount.sh failed"

CHROOT_PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin
in_chroot() {
    bb chroot "$R" /usr/bin/env -i HOME=/root LANG=C.UTF-8 PATH="$CHROOT_PATH" \
        DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive "$@"
}

# --- Network files (full setup only) ---------------------------------------
write_network_files() {
    ubu_log "writing resolv.conf, hosts, hostname"
    write_resolv_conf
    printf '%s\n' "$HOST" >"$R/etc/hostname"
    cat >"$R/etc/hosts" <<EOF
127.0.0.1   localhost
127.0.1.1   $HOST
::1         localhost ip6-localhost ip6-loopback
ff02::1     ip6-allnodes
ff02::2     ip6-allrouters
EOF
    # The kernel hostname is Android's (shared UTS namespace); keep it resolvable.
    _kh=$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/hostname 2>/dev/null) || _kh=""
    if [ -n "$_kh" ] && [ "$_kh" != "$HOST" ] && [ "$_kh" != localhost ]; then
        printf '127.0.1.1   %s\n' "$_kh" >>"$R/etc/hosts"
    fi
}

# --- Integration files ------------------------------------------------------------
# Everything below is UbuRoot's own and safe to rewrite: full setup and the
# migrations call these functions.
write_service_files() {
    printf 'LANG=C.UTF-8\n' >"$R/etc/default/locale"
    cat >"$R/usr/sbin/policy-rc.d" <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# UbuRoot: no init system in the chroot; never auto-start daemons on install.
exit 101
EOF
    chmod 755 "$R/usr/sbin/policy-rc.d"
    ubu_write_profile_hook "$R" || ubu_warn "could not write profile hook"
}

# --- Android paranoid-network groups --------------------------------------
# On Android kernels (CONFIG_ANDROID_PARANOID_NETWORK) these groups are more
# than socket permissions: membership in aid_net_raw acts as CAP_NET_RAW and
# aid_net_admin as CAP_NET_ADMIN. Seen on a device: a user in them could
# tcpdump the whole phone's traffic and add links in the host netns without
# sudo. So only root gets them; the user and _apt get aid_inet (sockets) only.
# Ping needs neither: Android's init.rc sets ping_group_range to all gids.
# Opt in by hand with: sudo usermod -aG aid_net_raw,aid_net_admin <user>
# Prints the group name that owns GID $2 (creating $1 with that GID if needed).
ensure_group() {
    _owner=$(bb awk -F: -v g="$2" '$3 == g { print $1; exit }' "$R/etc/group")
    if [ -n "$_owner" ]; then
        printf '%s' "$_owner"
        return 0
    fi
    in_chroot groupadd -g "$2" "$1" || return 1
    printf '%s' "$1"
}
# Removes $1 from group $2 if it is a member (older setups added it).
drop_from_group() {
    if bb awk -F: -v g="$2" -v u="$1" '$1 == g { n = split($4, m, ","); for (i = 1; i <= n; i++) if (m[i] == u) f = 1 } END { exit !f }' "$R/etc/group"; then
        in_chroot gpasswd -d "$1" "$2" >/dev/null && ubu_log "removed $1 from $2"
    fi
}
write_groups() {
    G_INET=$(ensure_group aid_inet 3003) || ubu_die "cannot create group aid_inet"
    G_RAW=$(ensure_group aid_net_raw 3004) || ubu_die "cannot create group aid_net_raw"
    G_ADMIN=$(ensure_group aid_net_admin 3005) || ubu_die "cannot create group aid_net_admin"
    AID_GROUPS="$G_INET,$G_RAW,$G_ADMIN"
    ubu_log "network groups: $AID_GROUPS (root), $G_INET (users)"
    # /sdcard is usually /data/media/0 (media_rw:media_rw 2770); see mount.sh.
    G_MEDIA=$(ensure_group aid_media_rw 1023) || ubu_die "cannot create group aid_media_rw"

    in_chroot usermod -a -G "$AID_GROUPS" root || ubu_warn "usermod root failed"
    if grep -q '^_apt:' "$R/etc/passwd"; then
        # APT drops privileges with setgroups(1, &pw_gid): only the PRIMARY group
        # survives, so _apt needs aid_inet as its primary group.
        in_chroot usermod -g "$G_INET" _apt || ubu_warn "usermod _apt failed"
        drop_from_group _apt "$G_RAW"
        drop_from_group _apt "$G_ADMIN"
    fi
}

# --- iptables backend ---------------------------------------------------------
# The chroot shares Android's network namespace, so iptables in Ubuntu edits
# the phone's firewall. Ubuntu defaults to iptables-nft, but Android's netd
# uses the legacy xtables (and kernels like 4.9 have no nf_tables at all), so
# point iptables/ip6tables at whichever backend holds Android's tables. An apt
# hook re-runs this after every dpkg run (iptables may be installed later);
# a choice the user made by hand (manual mode) is left alone.
write_iptables_select() {
    mkdir -p "$R/usr/local/sbin"
    cat >"$R/usr/local/sbin/uburoot-iptables-select" <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# UbuRoot: use the iptables backend that holds Android's rules (see setup.sh).
for t in iptables ip6tables; do
    [ -x "/usr/sbin/$t-legacy" ] || continue
    case "$t" in
        iptables) names=/proc/net/ip_tables_names ;;
        *) names=/proc/net/ip6_tables_names ;;
    esac
    # /proc files report size 0, so read them. Empty: Android is not on legacy.
    [ -n "$(cat "$names" 2>/dev/null)" ] || continue
    update-alternatives --query "$t" 2>/dev/null | grep -qx 'Status: auto' || continue
    update-alternatives --set "$t" "/usr/sbin/$t-legacy" >/dev/null &&
        echo "uburoot: $t -> $t-legacy (Android's tables; rules apply to the whole phone)"
done
exit 0
EOF
    chmod 755 "$R/usr/local/sbin/uburoot-iptables-select"
    cat >"$R/etc/apt/apt.conf.d/80uburoot-iptables" <<'EOF'
// Written by UbuRoot setup.sh: keep iptables on Android's backend.
DPkg::Post-Invoke { "if [ -x /usr/local/sbin/uburoot-iptables-select ]; then /usr/local/sbin/uburoot-iptables-select || true; fi"; };
EOF
    in_chroot /usr/local/sbin/uburoot-iptables-select || ubu_warn "uburoot-iptables-select failed"
}

# --- /etc/uburoot-release -------------------------------------------------------
write_release() {
    _f="$R/etc/uburoot-release"
    _inst=$(bb awk -F= '$1 == "INSTALLED" { print $2; exit }' "$_f" 2>/dev/null)
    if [ -z "$_inst" ]; then
        # A rootfs set up before this file existed: the setup marker's date.
        _inst=$(bb date -r "$R/etc/uburoot-setup.done" +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null) || _inst=$(date +%Y-%m-%d)
    fi
    _osr="$R/usr/lib/os-release"
    [ -f "$_osr" ] || _osr="$R/etc/os-release"
    _ubu=$(bb awk -F= '$1 == "VERSION_ID" || $1 == "VERSION_CODENAME" { gsub(/"/, "", $2); v = v (v == "" ? "" : " ") $2 } END { print v }' "$_osr")
    {
        printf '# Written by UbuRoot setup.sh. SCHEMA = version of the integration files.\n'
        printf 'SCHEMA=%s\n' "$UBU_SCHEMA"
        printf 'INSTALLED=%s\n' "$_inst"
        printf 'UBUNTU=%s\n' "$_ubu"
    } >"$_f.new" || ubu_die "cannot write $_f"
    mv "$_f.new" "$_f" || ubu_die "cannot write $_f"
}

# --- Migrations ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# migrate_N brings a rootfs from schema N-1 to N. Each must be idempotent and
# touch only UbuRoot's own files (never apt sources, users or packages).
# 0 -> 1: rootfs set up by 0.1.x/0.2.x, before /etc/uburoot-release existed.
# shellcheck disable=SC2317,SC2329 # called as "migrate_$_n" below (SC2317 in older shellcheck)
migrate_1() {
    write_service_files
    write_groups
    write_iptables_select
}

if [ "$MIGRATE" = 1 ]; then
    _n=$((FROM + 1))
    while [ "$_n" -le "$UBU_SCHEMA" ]; do
        ubu_log "migration $_n"
        "migrate_$_n" || ubu_die "migration $_n failed"
        _n=$((_n + 1))
    done
    write_release
    ubu_log "integration files: schema $FROM -> $UBU_SCHEMA"
    exit 0
fi

write_network_files
write_service_files
write_groups
write_iptables_select
write_sources

# --- Packages ----------------------------------------------------------------
if [ "$DO_APT" = 1 ]; then
    ubu_log "apt-get update"
    # Retry once with the _apt sandbox first: failures are often transient
    # (seen on a real device), and running downloads as root is the fallback.
    if ! in_chroot apt-get update && ! { sleep 3 && in_chroot apt-get update; }; then
        ubu_warn "apt-get update failed twice with the _apt sandbox; retrying as root"
        printf 'APT::Sandbox::User "root";\n' >"$R/etc/apt/apt.conf.d/01uburoot-sandbox"
        in_chroot apt-get update || ubu_die "apt-get update failed (network/DNS?)"
    fi
    ubu_log "installing sudo"
    in_chroot apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends sudo kmod || ubu_die "apt-get install sudo kmod failed"
fi

# --- User ------------------------------------------------------------------------
# netd's BPF firewall exempts only uid < 10000 (AID_APP_START). Higher uids are
# app uids and get dropped whenever an allowlist chain (restricted networking,
# doze, powersave) is on; 20000+ is also the SDK sandbox range since Android 13.
ubu_uid_ok() {
    case "$1" in '' | *[!0-9]*) return 1 ;; esac
    [ "$1" -ge 8000 ] && [ "$1" -le 9996 ]
}
# First id >= 9000 that is free both as a uid and as a gid.
ubu_free_id() {
    _id=9000
    while [ "$_id" -le 9996 ]; do
        if ! bb awk -F: -v i="$_id" '$3 == i { f = 1 } END { exit !f }' "$R/etc/passwd" "$R/etc/group"; then
            printf '%s' "$_id"
            return 0
        fi
        _id=$((_id + 1))
    done
    return 1
}
if [ -n "$NEW_USER" ]; then
    if grep -q "^$NEW_USER:" "$R/etc/passwd"; then
        ubu_log "user $NEW_USER already exists"
        _cur=$(bb awk -F: -v u="$NEW_USER" '$1 == u { print $3; exit }' "$R/etc/passwd")
        ubu_uid_ok "$_cur" ||
            ubu_warn "existing user $NEW_USER has uid $_cur (outside 8000-9996); network may be blocked"
    else
        if [ -z "$NEW_UID" ]; then
            NEW_UID=$(ubu_free_id) || ubu_die "no free uid/gid in 9000-9996"
        fi
        ubu_uid_ok "$NEW_UID" ||
            ubu_warn "uid $NEW_UID is outside 8000-9996: network may be blocked or it may clash with an Android AID"
        if ! grep -q "^$NEW_USER:" "$R/etc/group"; then
            in_chroot groupadd -g "$NEW_UID" "$NEW_USER" || ubu_die "groupadd $NEW_USER failed"
        fi
        in_chroot useradd -m -u "$NEW_UID" -g "$NEW_USER" -s /bin/bash "$NEW_USER" ||
            ubu_die "useradd $NEW_USER failed"
        ubu_log "created user $NEW_USER (uid $NEW_UID)"
    fi
    _groups="$G_INET,$G_MEDIA"
    grep -q '^sudo:' "$R/etc/group" && _groups="sudo,$_groups"
    in_chroot usermod -a -G "$_groups" "$NEW_USER" || ubu_warn "usermod $NEW_USER failed"
    drop_from_group "$NEW_USER" "$G_RAW"
    drop_from_group "$NEW_USER" "$G_ADMIN"
    if [ -n "$USER_PW" ]; then
        printf '%s:%s\n' "$NEW_USER" "$USER_PW" | bb chroot "$R" /usr/bin/env -i \
            PATH="$CHROOT_PATH" chpasswd || ubu_warn "setting the password failed"
    fi
    _sudoers="$R/etc/sudoers.d/90-uburoot-$NEW_USER"
    if [ "$NOPASSWD" = 1 ]; then
        mkdir -p "$R/etc/sudoers.d"
        printf '%s ALL=(ALL:ALL) NOPASSWD: ALL\n' "$NEW_USER" >"$_sudoers" && chmod 440 "$_sudoers"
    else
        rm -f "$_sudoers"
    fi
fi

date >"$R/etc/uburoot-setup.done" 2>/dev/null
write_release
ubu_log "setup complete"
exit 0

```

`app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/umount.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Unmount everything at or below ROOTFS, deepest first, reading /proc/self/mounts.
# Falls back to lazy umount (-l). Exit 0 only if nothing is left mounted.
# Usage: umount.sh [--kill]
#   --kill  first terminate processes whose root is inside the chroot
set -u
UBU_BIN_DIR=$(cd -P -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)
# shellcheck source=common.sh
. "$UBU_BIN_DIR/common.sh"

ubu_require_root
ubu_init

KILL=0
for _a in "$@"; do
    case "$_a" in
        --kill) KILL=1 ;;
        *) ubu_die "unknown option: $_a" ;;
    esac
done

R=$ROOTFS

# uburoot-hw's rule watcher runs inside the chroot by itself: stop it even
# without --kill, or it would keep the rootfs busy.
_w=$(cat "$R/run/uburoot/hw-watch.pid" 2>/dev/null)
if [ -n "$_w" ] && [ "$(readlink "/proc/$_w/root" 2>/dev/null)" = "$R" ]; then
    kill "$_w" 2>/dev/null
fi

if [ "$KILL" = 1 ]; then
    _pids=$(ubu_chroot_pids)
    if [ -n "$_pids" ]; then
        ubu_log "stopping chroot processes: $(printf '%s' "$_pids" | tr '\n' ' ')"
        # HUP too: interactive shells ignore TERM but exit on a hangup.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2086 # word splitting of the pid list is intended
        kill -HUP $_pids 2>/dev/null
        # shellcheck disable=SC2086
        kill -TERM $_pids 2>/dev/null
        _n=0
        while [ $_n -lt 10 ] && [ -n "$(ubu_chroot_pids)" ]; do
            sleep 0.3 2>/dev/null || sleep 1
            _n=$((_n + 1))
        done
        _pids=$(ubu_chroot_pids)
        # shellcheck disable=SC2086
        [ -n "$_pids" ] && kill -KILL $_pids 2>/dev/null
    fi
fi

# Cut every propagation link first, so no umount below can reach the real
# mounts (an rbind copy of /apex that is still shared would otherwise
# unmount the real /apex/* submounts).
# --make-rprivate is recursive, so only the topmost mounts need it (normally
# just the rootfs self-bind). The check right below still aborts if anything
# under the rootfs is left shared.
_top=""
for _mp in $(ubu_mounts_under "$R" | bb sort -u); do
    case "$_mp" in *\\*) _mp=$(ubu_unescape "$_mp") ;; esac
    _covered=0
    for _t in $_top; do
        case "$_mp" in "$_t"/*) _covered=1 ;; esac
    done
    [ "$_covered" = 1 ] && continue
    bb mount --make-rprivate "$_mp" 2>/dev/null || bb mount -o rprivate "$_mp" 2>/dev/null
    _top="$_top $_mp"
done
if ubu_any_shared_under "$R"; then
    ubu_die "mounts under $R are still shared and propagation could not be changed; NOT unmounting (reboot to clear)"
fi

# The Android partitions are rbind trees. With Magisk's Zygisk (magic mount)
# /system/lib and /system/lib64 alone hold ~950 per-file mounts, and
# unmounting them one by one took 127 s on the test phone. A lazy umount
# (MNT_DETACH) is recursive: one syscall drops a whole tree. It is safe here
# because everything under the rootfs is private now, so nothing propagates.
for _p in $UBU_ANDROID_PARTS; do
    if ubu_is_mounted "$R$_p"; then
        bb umount -l "$R$_p" 2>/dev/null || ubu_warn "could not detach $R$_p"
    fi
done

_pass=0
while [ $_pass -lt 5 ]; do
    _list=$(ubu_mounts_under "$R")
    [ -z "$_list" ] && break
    for _mp in $_list; do
        case "$_mp" in *\\*) _mp=$(ubu_unescape "$_mp") ;; esac
        ubu_is_mounted "$_mp" || continue
        if ! bb umount "$_mp" 2>/dev/null; then
            bb umount -l "$_mp" 2>/dev/null || ubu_warn "could not unmount $_mp"
        fi
    done
    _pass=$((_pass + 1))
done

_left=$(ubu_mounts_under "$R")
if [ -n "$_left" ]; then
    ubu_log "ERROR: still mounted under $R:"
    printf '%s\n' "$_left" | sed 's/^/[uburoot]   /' >&2
    exit 1
fi
ubu_log "all mounts under $R released"
exit 0

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/UbuRootApp.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot

import android.app.Application
import com.topjohnwu.superuser.Shell

class UbuRootApp : Application() {
    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        // Every root call runs in the global mount namespace (CLAUDE.md:
        // "always pass --mount-master"); libsu builds `su --mount-master`.
        Shell.setDefaultBuilder(
            Shell.Builder.create()
                .setFlags(Shell.FLAG_MOUNT_MASTER)
                .setTimeout(60),
        )
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/bridge/CommandReceiver.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.bridge

import android.app.Activity
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.ActivityNotFoundException
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.ClipData
import android.content.ClipboardManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Build
import android.os.VibrationEffect
import android.os.Vibrator
import android.os.VibratorManager
import android.util.Log
import android.webkit.MimeTypeMap
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.NotificationManagerCompat
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import androidx.core.net.toUri
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.R
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.RootShell
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.Sh
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.UbuPaths
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.ui.MainActivity
import java.io.File
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
import kotlinx.coroutines.MainScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull

/**
 * The Android end of the uburoot-* commands. Ubuntu sends them with
 * `am broadcast` through the root helper uburoot-send (chroot-send.sh): the
 * receiver is protected by a signature permission that only root, the system
 * and this app pass. `am broadcast` is ordered, so the answer goes back to the
 * terminal: RESULT_OK, or result 1 with the error message as result data.
 */
class CommandReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {
    override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent) {
        if (intent.action != ACTION) return
        val app = context.applicationContext
        val cmd = intent.getStringExtra("cmd")
        if (cmd == "open" && intent.getStringExtra("url") == null) {
            // Taking the file from the outbox needs root: off the main thread,
            // with the broadcast (and its background-activity-start grant) kept
            // open until the viewer has started.
            val pending = goAsync()
            scope.launch {
                // null = timed out; the Result holds null (done) or the error text.
                val outcome = withTimeoutOrNull(OPEN_TIMEOUT_MS) { runCatching { openFile(app, intent) } }
                val error = if (outcome == null) {
                    app.getString(R.string.bridge_timeout)
                } else {
                    outcome.fold({ it }, ::message)
                }
                pending.resultCode = if (error == null) Activity.RESULT_OK else RESULT_ERROR
                pending.resultData = error
                pending.finish()
            }
            return
        }
        val error = runCatching { handle(app, cmd, intent) }.fold({ it }, ::message)
        resultCode = if (error == null) Activity.RESULT_OK else RESULT_ERROR
        resultData = error
    }

    private fun message(e: Throwable): String {
        Log.w(TAG, "bridge command failed", e)
        return e.message ?: e.toString()
    }

    /** null = done, else the error shown in the terminal. */
    private fun handle(app: Context, cmd: String?, intent: Intent): String? = when (cmd) {
        "notify" -> notify(app, intent.getStringExtra("title").orEmpty(), intent.getStringExtra("text").orEmpty())

        "toast" -> {
            Toast.makeText(app, intent.getStringExtra("text").orEmpty(), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            null
        }

        "vibrate" -> vibrate(app, intent.getStringExtra("ms")?.toLongOrNull()?.coerceIn(1, MAX_VIBRATE_MS) ?: 300)

        "clip" -> {
            val cm = app.getSystemService(ClipboardManager::class.java)
            cm.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("UbuRoot", intent.getStringExtra("text").orEmpty()))
            null
        }

        "open" -> openUrl(app, intent.getStringExtra("url").orEmpty())

        else -> app.getString(R.string.bridge_unknown_command, cmd.orEmpty())
    }

    private fun notify(app: Context, title: String, text: String): String? {
        val nm = NotificationManagerCompat.from(app)
        if (!nm.areNotificationsEnabled()) return app.getString(R.string.bridge_notifications_off)
        app.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(
            NotificationChannel(
                CHANNEL,
                app.getString(R.string.bridge_channel),
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT,
            ),
        )
        if (nm.getNotificationChannelCompat(CHANNEL)?.importance == NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_NONE) {
            return app.getString(R.string.bridge_notifications_off)
        }
        val tap = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            app,
            0,
            Intent(app, MainActivity::class.java).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK),
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE,
        )
        val n = NotificationCompat.Builder(app, CHANNEL)
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_notification)
            .setContentTitle(title)
            .setContentText(text.ifEmpty { null })
            .setStyle(NotificationCompat.BigTextStyle().bigText(text))
            .setContentIntent(tap)
            .setAutoCancel(true)
            .build()
        @Suppress("MissingPermission") // checked: areNotificationsEnabled()
        nm.notify(nextId.incrementAndGet(), n)
        return null
    }

    private fun vibrate(app: Context, ms: Long): String? {
        val vibrator = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            app.getSystemService(VibratorManager::class.java).defaultVibrator
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            app.getSystemService(Vibrator::class.java)
        }
        if (vibrator == null || !vibrator.hasVibrator()) return app.getString(R.string.bridge_no_vibrator)
        vibrator.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(ms, VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE))
        return null
    }

    private fun openUrl(app: Context, url: String): String? {
        val uri = url.toUri()
        if (uri.scheme.isNullOrEmpty()) return app.getString(R.string.bridge_bad_url, url)
        return view(app, Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, uri), url)
    }

    /** Copies the outbox file into our cache and hands it to a viewer through FileProvider. */
    private suspend fun openFile(app: Context, intent: Intent): String? {
        val id = intent.getStringExtra("id").orEmpty()
        if (!ID.matches(id)) return app.getString(R.string.bridge_bad_request)
        val name = SharedPaths.safeName(intent.getStringExtra("name"))
        val root = File(app.cacheDir, OPEN_DIR)
        pruneOld(root)
        val dir = File(root, id)
        dir.mkdirs()
        val file = File(dir, name)
        // Created by the app, so owner and SELinux label are ours; root only writes into it.
        file.createNewFile()
        val r = RootShell.run("${UbuPaths.script("bridge.sh")} take ${Sh.quote(id)} ${Sh.quote(file.absolutePath)}")
        if (!r.ok) {
            dir.deleteRecursively()
            return app.getString(R.string.bridge_take_failed, r.text)
        }
        val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(app, "${app.packageName}.files", file)
        val view = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).setDataAndType(uri, mimeOf(name, intent.getStringExtra("mime")))
            .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
        return view(app, view, name)
    }

    private fun view(app: Context, view: Intent, what: String): String? = try {
        app.startActivity(view.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK))
        null
    } catch (_: ActivityNotFoundException) {
        app.getString(R.string.bridge_no_app, what)
    }

    companion object {
        const val ACTION = "com.reinanbr.uburoot.CMD"
        private const val TAG = "UbuRoot"
        private const val CHANNEL = "messages"
        private const val OPEN_DIR = "open"
        private const val RESULT_ERROR = 1
        private const val MAX_VIBRATE_MS = 10_000L
        private const val OPEN_TIMEOUT_MS = 8_000L // a foreground broadcast gets 10 s
        private const val KEEP_MS = 24 * 3600 * 1000L
        private val ID = Regex("[0-9a-f]{16}")
        private val nextId = AtomicInteger(1000)
        private val scope = MainScope()

        /** The extension's type is what viewers register for; `file`'s guess comes second. */
        fun mimeOf(name: String, sniffed: String?): String {
            val ext = name.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase()
            return MimeTypeMap.getSingleton().getMimeTypeFromExtension(ext)
                ?: sniffed?.takeIf { MIME.matches(it) && it != "application/octet-stream" }
                ?: "application/octet-stream"
        }

        private val MIME = Regex("[a-z]+/[a-zA-Z0-9.+_-]+")

        /** Files handed to viewers stay a day: the viewer may read them long after the start. */
        private fun pruneOld(root: File) {
            val old = System.currentTimeMillis() - KEEP_MS
            root.listFiles()?.filter { it.lastModified() < old }?.forEach { it.deleteRecursively() }
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/bridge/ShareActivity.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.bridge

import android.content.ContentResolver
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.provider.DocumentsContract
import android.provider.MediaStore
import android.provider.OpenableColumns
import android.util.Log
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.padding
import androidx.compose.material3.CircularProgressIndicator
import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.core.content.IntentCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.R
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.RootShell
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.Sh
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.UbuPaths
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.terminal.SessionManager
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.terminal.TerminalActivity
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.ui.MainActivity
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.ui.UbuRootTheme
import java.io.File
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/**
 * "Open in UbuRoot" share target: a new session in the shared file's directory.
 * Files on the phone's shared storage are already in Ubuntu (/sdcard); anything
 * else (another app's private file, an SD card, a cloud file) is copied into
 * ~/Shared of the default user first.
 */
class ShareActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContent {
            UbuRootTheme {
                Surface(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    Column(
                        Modifier.padding(24.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp, Alignment.CenterVertically),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                    ) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator()
                        Text(stringResource(R.string.share_working))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (savedInstanceState == null) lifecycleScope.launch { open(intent) }
    }

    private suspend fun open(intent: Intent) {
        val cwd = runCatching { cwdFor(intent) }.getOrElse {
            Log.w(TAG, "share failed", it)
            Toast.makeText(
                this,
                getString(R.string.share_failed, it.message ?: it.toString()),
                Toast.LENGTH_LONG,
            ).show()
            null
        }
        // Live sessions mean the preflight already passed and the rootfs is in place.
        startActivity(
            if (SessionManager.sessions.isNotEmpty()) {
                TerminalActivity.intent(this, newSession = cwd != null, cwd = cwd)
            } else {
                MainActivity.intent(this, cwd)
            },
        )
        finish()
    }

    /** The directory inside Ubuntu for the shared item, importing it when it isn't there. */
    private suspend fun cwdFor(intent: Intent): String {
        val stream = IntentCompat.getParcelableExtra(intent, Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, Uri::class.java)
        if (stream == null) {
            // Plain text (a link, a snippet): saved as a file too.
            val text = intent.getStringExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT) ?: error(getString(R.string.share_nothing))
            return import(getString(R.string.share_text_name)) { it.writeText(text) }
        }
        val dir = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { locate(stream) }
        if (dir != null && RootShell.run("${UbuPaths.script("bridge.sh")} cwd ${Sh.quote(dir)}").ok) return dir
        return import(SharedPaths.safeName(displayName(stream))) { f ->
            val input = contentResolver.openInputStream(stream) ?: error(getString(R.string.share_nothing))
            input.use { i -> f.outputStream().use { i.copyTo(it) } }
        }
    }

    /** Copies the item into our cache, then into ~/Shared through bridge.sh; returns that directory. */
    private suspend fun import(name: String, write: (File) -> Unit): String {
        val tmp = File(cacheDir, "share").apply { mkdirs() }.let { File(it, "incoming") }
        try {
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { write(tmp) }
            val r = RootShell.run(
                "${UbuPaths.script("bridge.sh")} import ${Sh.quote(tmp.absolutePath)} ${Sh.quote(name)}",
            )
            val dir = r.out.lastOrNull()?.trim()
            if (!r.ok || dir == null || !dir.startsWith("/")) error(r.text)
            return dir
        } finally {
            tmp.delete()
        }
    }

    /** Where the file is on the phone's shared storage, as a path inside Ubuntu (null: not there). */
    private fun locate(uri: Uri): String? {
        if (uri.scheme == ContentResolver.SCHEME_FILE) return uri.path?.let(SharedPaths::fromAndroidPath)
        if (uri.scheme != ContentResolver.SCHEME_CONTENT) return null
        if (DocumentsContract.isDocumentUri(this, uri)) {
            val id = DocumentsContract.getDocumentId(uri)
            when (uri.authority) {
                "com.android.externalstorage.documents" -> return SharedPaths.fromExternalStorageDocId(id)
                "com.android.providers.downloads.documents" -> SharedPaths.fromDownloadsDocId(id)?.let { return it }
            }
        }
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            query(uri, MediaStore.MediaColumns.VOLUME_NAME, MediaStore.MediaColumns.RELATIVE_PATH)?.let { (vol, rel) ->
                SharedPaths.fromMediaStore(vol, rel)?.let { return it }
            }
        }
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION") // still filled in by MediaStore and many file managers
        return query(uri, MediaStore.MediaColumns.DATA)?.firstOrNull()?.let(SharedPaths::fromAndroidPath)
    }

    /** The columns' values for [uri], or null when the provider doesn't have them. */
    private fun query(uri: Uri, vararg columns: String): List<String?>? = runCatching {
        contentResolver.query(uri, columns, null, null, null)?.use { c ->
            if (!c.moveToFirst()) return@use null
            columns.map { col -> c.getColumnIndex(col).takeIf { it >= 0 }?.let(c::getString) }
                .takeIf { values -> values.any { it != null } }
        }
    }.getOrNull()

    private fun displayName(uri: Uri): String? =
        query(uri, OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME)?.firstOrNull() ?: uri.lastPathSegment

    private companion object {
        const val TAG = "UbuRoot"
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/bridge/SharedPaths.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.bridge

import android.annotation.SuppressLint

/**
 * Maps where Android says a shared file lives to its directory inside Ubuntu.
 * Only the phone's primary shared storage is visible there (as /sdcard), so
 * anything else gives null and the file is imported instead (bridge.sh import).
 */
@SuppressLint("SdCardPath") // paths inside Ubuntu and Android's names for user 0's storage, not app storage
object SharedPaths {
    const val SDCARD = "/sdcard"

    // How Android spells the primary shared storage (user 0).
    private val ANDROID_ROOTS = listOf(
        "/storage/emulated/0",
        "/storage/self/primary",
        "/sdcard",
        "/data/media/0",
        "/mnt/user/0/emulated/0",
    )

    /** "primary:Download/a.pdf" (ExternalStorageProvider) -> "/sdcard/Download". */
    fun fromExternalStorageDocId(docId: String): String? {
        val colon = docId.indexOf(':')
        if (colon < 0) return null
        val rel = docId.substring(colon + 1)
        return when (docId.substring(0, colon)) {
            "primary" -> parentDir(SDCARD, rel)
            "home" -> parentDir("$SDCARD/Documents", rel)
            else -> null
        }
    }

    /** "raw:/storage/emulated/0/Download/a.pdf" (DownloadsProvider) and plain paths. */
    fun fromDownloadsDocId(docId: String): String? =
        if (docId.startsWith("raw:")) fromAndroidPath(docId.removePrefix("raw:")) else null

    /** "/storage/emulated/0/Download/a.pdf" -> "/sdcard/Download". */
    fun fromAndroidPath(path: String): String? {
        for (root in ANDROID_ROOTS) {
            if (path.startsWith("$root/")) return parentDir(SDCARD, path.removePrefix("$root/"))
        }
        return null
    }

    /** MediaStore's VOLUME_NAME + RELATIVE_PATH ("Download/Sub/") -> "/sdcard/Download/Sub". */
    fun fromMediaStore(volume: String?, relativePath: String?): String? {
        if (relativePath == null || volume !in setOf(null, "external", "external_primary")) return null
        val rel = relativePath.trim('/')
        if (!safe(rel)) return null
        return if (rel.isEmpty()) SDCARD else "$SDCARD/$rel"
    }

    /** Directory of [rel] (a file path relative to [base]); null on "..", "." or empty parts. */
    private fun parentDir(base: String, rel: String): String? {
        val dir = rel.trim('/').substringBeforeLast('/', "")
        if (!safe(dir)) return null
        return if (dir.isEmpty()) base else "$base/$dir"
    }

    private fun safe(rel: String) = rel.isEmpty() ||
        (rel.none { it < ' ' } && rel.split('/').none { it.isEmpty() || it == "." || it == ".." })

    /** A file name that is safe as one path component. */
    fun safeName(name: String?): String {
        val n = name?.substringAfterLast('/')?.replace(Regex("[\\u0000-\\u001f]"), "")?.trim().orEmpty()
        return if (n.isEmpty() || n == "." || n == "..") "shared-file" else n.take(200)
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/hardware/HardwareStatus.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.hardware

/** One hardware class from `hardware.sh status`: `class=NAME|ROUTE|STATE|NODES|GROUP|USERS`. */
data class HwClass(
    val name: String,
    /** direct, bridge, takeover, read or none. */
    val route: String,
    /** available, missing, taken, unsupported or bridge. */
    val state: String,
    val nodes: Int,
    /** Ubuntu group that grants it (empty: none). */
    val group: String,
    val users: List<String>,
)

/** A cpufreq policy: `cpu=POLICY|CPUS|GOVERNOR|MIN|MAX|HWMAX` (kHz). */
data class CpuPolicy(
    val name: String,
    val cpus: String,
    val governor: String,
    val min: Int,
    val max: Int,
    val hwMax: Int,
)

/** Parsed output of `hardware.sh status`. */
data class HardwareStatus(
    val access: Boolean,
    val classes: List<HwClass>,
    val cpus: List<CpuPolicy>,
    /** "85|Charging|312" = percent, status, tenths of a degree; null if there is no battery. */
    val battery: Triple<Int?, String, Int?>?,
    val wakeLock: Boolean,
    val changes: Int,
) {
    val wifiTaken get() = classes.any { it.name == "wifi" && it.state == "taken" }

    companion object {
        fun parse(lines: List<String>): HardwareStatus {
            var access = false
            val classes = mutableListOf<HwClass>()
            val cpus = mutableListOf<CpuPolicy>()
            var battery: Triple<Int?, String, Int?>? = null
            var wake = false
            var changes = 0
            for (line in lines) {
                val i = line.indexOf('=')
                if (i <= 0) continue
                val value = line.substring(i + 1)
                when (line.substring(0, i)) {
                    "access" -> access = value == "on"

                    "class" -> {
                        val f = value.split('|')
                        if (f.size >= 6) {
                            classes += HwClass(
                                name = f[0],
                                route = f[1],
                                state = f[2],
                                nodes = f[3].toIntOrNull() ?: 0,
                                group = f[4],
                                users = f[5].split(',').filter { it.isNotBlank() },
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    "cpu" -> {
                        val f = value.split('|')
                        if (f.size >= 6) {
                            cpus += CpuPolicy(
                                f[0],
                                f[1],
                                f[2],
                                f[3].toIntOrNull() ?: 0,
                                f[4].toIntOrNull() ?: 0,
                                f[5].toIntOrNull() ?: 0,
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    "battery" -> {
                        val f = value.split('|')
                        battery =
                            Triple(f.getOrNull(0)?.toIntOrNull(), f.getOrElse(1) { "" }, f.getOrNull(2)?.toIntOrNull())
                    }

                    "wakelock" -> wake = value == "on"

                    "changes" -> changes = value.toIntOrNull() ?: 0
                }
            }
            return HardwareStatus(access, classes, cpus, battery, wake, changes)
        }
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/hardware/HardwareViewModel.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.hardware

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.install.ConfigStore
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.RootShell
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.Sh
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.UbuPaths
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class HardwareState(
    val loading: Boolean = true,
    val status: HardwareStatus? = null,
    /** DEFAULT_USER from uburoot.conf; empty = root (needs no grants). */
    val user: String = "",
    val busy: Boolean = false,
    /** Output of the last action (hardware.sh's own messages). */
    val output: String = "",
    /** `hardware.sh info CLASS` of the class the user opened. */
    val info: Pair<String, String>? = null,
)

/**
 * The Hardware screen: everything goes through hardware.sh on the Android side, which
 * records each change in the same log as kernel.sh (Stop all reverts both). The app
 * asks for confirmation itself, so the commands get -y.
 */
class HardwareViewModel(app: Application) : AndroidViewModel(app) {
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(HardwareState())
    val state: StateFlow<HardwareState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private val hw = UbuPaths.script("hardware.sh")

    fun refresh() = viewModelScope.launch { reload() }

    private suspend fun reload() {
        val status = HardwareStatus.parse(RootShell.run("$hw status").out)
        val user = runCatching { ConfigStore.read().defaultUser }.getOrDefault("")
        _state.update { it.copy(loading = false, status = status, user = user) }
    }

    fun grant(cls: String, on: Boolean) {
        val user = _state.value.user
        if (user.isEmpty()) return
        act("$hw -y ${if (on) "grant" else "revoke"} ${Sh.quote(cls)} ${Sh.quote(user)}")
    }

    fun wifi(take: Boolean) = act("$hw -y ${if (take) "takeover" else "release"} wifi")

    fun wakeLock(on: Boolean) = act("$hw -y wakelock ${if (on) "on" else "off"}")

    /** The same revert as Stop all: every recorded kernel and hardware change. */
    fun revert() = act("${UbuPaths.script("kernel.sh")} -y revert")

    fun info(cls: String) = viewModelScope.launch {
        val text = RootShell.run("$hw info ${Sh.quote(cls)}").text
        _state.update { it.copy(info = cls to text.trim()) }
    }

    fun closeInfo() = _state.update { it.copy(info = null) }

    private fun act(cmd: String) {
        if (_state.value.busy) return
        _state.update { it.copy(busy = true, output = "") }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val out = try {
                RootShell.run(cmd).text
            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName
            }
            reload()
            _state.update { it.copy(busy = false, output = out.trim()) }
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/install/ConfigStore.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.install

import android.content.Context
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.RootShell
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.Sh
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.UbuPaths
import java.io.File
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/** Reads and writes uburoot.conf (root:root 644 in /data/local/uburoot, so through su). */
object ConfigStore {
    suspend fun read(): UbuConfig {
        val r = RootShell.run("[ ! -e ${UbuPaths.CONFIG} ] || cat ${UbuPaths.CONFIG}")
        if (!r.ok) error("cannot read ${UbuPaths.CONFIG}: ${r.text}")
        return UbuConfig.parse(r.text)
    }

    suspend fun write(context: Context, config: UbuConfig) {
        val tmp = File(context.cacheDir, "uburoot.conf")
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { tmp.writeText(config.render()) }
        // Written next to the target and renamed: a script sourcing it never sees half a file.
        val r = RootShell.run(
            "cp -f ${Sh.quote(tmp.absolutePath)} ${UbuPaths.CONFIG}.new && " +
                "chown 0:0 ${UbuPaths.CONFIG}.new && chmod 644 ${UbuPaths.CONFIG}.new && " +
                "mv -f ${UbuPaths.CONFIG}.new ${UbuPaths.CONFIG}",
        )
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { tmp.delete() }
        if (!r.ok) error("cannot write ${UbuPaths.CONFIG}: ${r.text}")
    }

    /** Ubuntu accounts a session can log in as: root plus the regular users (uid 1000..59999). */
    suspend fun users(): List<String> {
        val r = RootShell.run(
            "awk -F: '\$3 >= 1000 && \$3 < 60000 { print \$1 }' ${UbuPaths.ROOTFS}/etc/passwd",
        )
        return listOf("root") + if (r.ok) r.out.filter { it.isNotBlank() } else emptyList()
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/install/Downloader.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.install

import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.io.IOException
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.security.MessageDigest
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.currentCoroutineContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.ensureActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/**
 * Resumable HTTP download: bytes go to `<dest>.part`, a later call continues
 * with a Range request, and the file is renamed to [dest] only after its
 * SHA256 matches.
 */
class Downloader(private val open: (String) -> HttpURLConnection = Http::open) {

    suspend fun download(
        url: String,
        dest: File,
        sha256: String,
        onProgress: (done: Long, total: Long) -> Unit,
    ): File = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        if (dest.isFile && sha256Of(dest) == sha256) return@withContext dest
        dest.parentFile?.mkdirs()
        val part = File(dest.path + ".part")
        // Two attempts: a resumed .part from a different upstream file fails
        // the checksum once, then we start over from zero.
        for (attempt in 1..2) {
            fetch(url, part, onProgress)
            if (sha256Of(part) == sha256) {
                dest.delete()
                if (!part.renameTo(dest)) throw IOException("cannot rename ${part.name}")
                return@withContext dest
            }
            part.delete()
        }
        throw IOException("SHA256 mismatch for ${dest.name}")
    }

    private suspend fun fetch(url: String, part: File, onProgress: (Long, Long) -> Unit) {
        var have = if (part.isFile) part.length() else 0L
        val c = open(url)
        try {
            if (have > 0) c.setRequestProperty("Range", "bytes=$have-")
            val total = when (c.responseCode) {
                HttpURLConnection.HTTP_PARTIAL ->
                    c.getHeaderField("Content-Range")?.substringAfterLast('/')?.toLongOrNull() ?: -1L

                HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK -> {
                    have = 0 // server ignored the Range header: start over
                    c.contentLengthLong
                }

                416 -> return

                // nothing left to fetch; the checksum decides
                else -> throw HttpException(c.responseCode, url)
            }
            c.inputStream.use { input ->
                FileOutputStream(part, have > 0).use { out ->
                    val buf = ByteArray(64 * 1024)
                    var done = have
                    var lastReport = 0L
                    onProgress(done, total)
                    while (true) {
                        currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive()
                        val n = input.read(buf)
                        if (n < 0) break
                        out.write(buf, 0, n)
                        done += n
                        if (done - lastReport >= 256 * 1024) {
                            lastReport = done
                            onProgress(done, total)
                        }
                    }
                    onProgress(done, total)
                }
            }
        } finally {
            c.disconnect()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        fun sha256Of(file: File): String {
            val md = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
            file.inputStream().use { input ->
                val buf = ByteArray(64 * 1024)
                while (true) {
                    val n = input.read(buf)
                    if (n < 0) break
                    md.update(buf, 0, n)
                }
            }
            return md.digest().joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
        }
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/install/Http.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.install

import java.io.IOException
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

internal object Http {
    // Generic client name: no device or user details leave the phone.
    const val USER_AGENT = "UbuRoot"

    fun open(url: String): HttpURLConnection = (URL(url).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
        connectTimeout = 20_000
        readTimeout = 30_000
        instanceFollowRedirects = true
        setRequestProperty("User-Agent", USER_AGENT)
    }

    fun getText(url: String): String {
        val c = open(url)
        try {
            if (c.responseCode != HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK) {
                throw HttpException(c.responseCode, url)
            }
            return c.inputStream.bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
        } finally {
            c.disconnect()
        }
    }
}

class HttpException(val code: Int, url: String) : IOException("HTTP $code: $url")
```
