`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/install/InstallViewModel.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.install

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.app.Application
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.PowerManager
import android.provider.OpenableColumns
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.RootInfo
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.RootShell
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.ScriptDeployer
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.Sh
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.UbuPaths
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.terminal.SessionService
import java.io.File
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/** Why the app cannot continue; the UI maps each one to a message. */
enum class Problem { NO_ROOT, NOT_GLOBAL_NAMESPACE, RESTRICTED_ROOT, NOT_ARM64, NO_SPACE, DEPLOY_FAILED }

enum class Step { DISCOVER, DOWNLOAD, COPY, EXTRACT, SETUP, MIGRATE }

sealed interface Stage {
    data object Checking : Stage
    data class Blocked(val problem: Problem, val info: RootInfo?, val detail: String = "") : Stage
    data class Form(val info: RootInfo) : Stage
    data class Working(val step: Step, val progress: Float? = null, val detail: String = "") : Stage
    data class Failed(val message: String) : Stage
    data object Ready : Stage
}

/** A rootfs archive the user picked (SAF) instead of the download; [sha256] may be empty. */
data class LocalArchive(val uri: Uri, val name: String, val sha256: String)

data class InstallRequest(
    val user: String,
    val password: String,
    val nopasswd: Boolean,
    /** http:// ports mirror, empty = ports.ubuntu.com (APT_MIRROR). */
    val mirror: String = "",
    val local: LocalArchive? = null,
)

class InstallViewModel(app: Application) : AndroidViewModel(app) {
    private val _stage = MutableStateFlow<Stage>(Stage.Checking)
    val stage: StateFlow<Stage> = _stage.asStateFlow()

    private val _log = MutableStateFlow<List<String>>(emptyList())
    val log: StateFlow<List<String>> = _log.asStateFlow()

    private val downloadDir get() = File(getApplication<Application>().filesDir, "download")

    init {
        check()
    }

    fun check() {
        _stage.value = Stage.Checking
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _stage.value = preflight()
        }
    }

    private suspend fun preflight(): Stage {
        // A "Stop all" still unmounting: opening a session now would race mount.sh against umount.sh.
        SessionService.stopping.first { !it }
        if (!RootShell.isRoot()) return Stage.Blocked(Problem.NO_ROOT, null)
        val deployed = ScriptDeployer.deploy(getApplication())
        if (!deployed.ok) return Stage.Blocked(Problem.DEPLOY_FAILED, null, deployed.text)
        val info = ScriptDeployer.rootInfo()
        // A run killed between install.sh and its cleanup leaves the tarball.
        if (info.rootfsInstalled) withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { downloadDir.deleteRecursively() }
        return when {
            !info.globalNamespace -> Stage.Blocked(Problem.NOT_GLOBAL_NAMESPACE, info)

            !info.fullRoot -> Stage.Blocked(Problem.RESTRICTED_ROOT, info, info.rootDetail)

            !info.isArm64 -> Stage.Blocked(Problem.NOT_ARM64, info, info.arch)

            info.needsMigration -> migrate(info)

            info.rootfsInstalled && info.setupDone -> Stage.Ready

            !info.rootfsInstalled && (info.freeBytes ?: Long.MAX_VALUE) < MIN_FREE_BYTES ->
                Stage.Blocked(Problem.NO_SPACE, info)

            else -> Stage.Form(info)
        }
    }

    /** A newer app version brings integration files the installed rootfs doesn't have yet. */
    private suspend fun migrate(info: RootInfo): Stage {
        _log.value = emptyList()
        _stage.value = Stage.Working(Step.MIGRATE, detail = "${info.schema} → ${info.schemaCurrent}")
        val code = RootShell.stream("${UbuPaths.script("setup.sh")} --migrate", ::appendLog)
        return if (code == 0) Stage.Ready else Stage.Failed("setup.sh --migrate failed (exit $code)")
    }

    fun install(req: InstallRequest, info: RootInfo) {
        if (!UbuConfig.isValidUserName(req.user)) return
        if (req.mirror.isNotEmpty() && !UbuConfig.isValidMirror(req.mirror)) return
        _log.value = emptyList()
        viewModelScope.launch {
            // Android may stop an app whose screen goes off mid-install (NetHunter warns
            // about it too): the screen stays on while working (MainActivity) and the
            // CPU stays awake with this lock.
            val lock = wakeLock()
            _stage.value = try {
                runInstall(req, info)
                Stage.Ready
            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                appendLog("ERROR: ${e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName}")
                Stage.Failed(e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)
            } finally {
                if (lock.isHeld) lock.release()
            }
        }
    }

    @SuppressLint("WakelockTimeout") // it has one
    private fun wakeLock(): PowerManager.WakeLock = getApplication<Application>()
        .getSystemService(PowerManager::class.java)
        .newWakeLock(PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK, "uburoot:install")
        .apply {
            setReferenceCounted(false)
            acquire(WAKE_LOCK_MS)
        }

    private suspend fun runInstall(req: InstallRequest, info: RootInfo) {
        if (!info.rootfsInstalled && req.local != null) {
            val archive = copyLocal(req.local)
            _stage.value = Stage.Working(Step.EXTRACT)
            val sha = req.local.sha256.lowercase()
            val code = RootShell.stream(
                "${UbuPaths.script("install.sh")} ${Sh.quote(archive.absolutePath)} ${Sh.quote(sha)}",
                ::appendLog,
            )
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { downloadDir.deleteRecursively() }
            if (code != 0) error("install.sh failed (exit $code)")
        } else if (!info.rootfsInstalled) {
            _stage.value = Stage.Working(Step.DISCOVER)
            val release = ReleaseDiscovery.discover()
            appendLog("Ubuntu ${release.version}: ${release.url}")
            appendLog("sha256 ${release.sha256}")

            val tarball = File(downloadDir, release.fileName)
            _stage.value = Stage.Working(Step.DOWNLOAD, 0f, release.version)
            Downloader().download(release.url, tarball, release.sha256) { done, total ->
                val p = if (total > 0) done.toFloat() / total else null
                _stage.value = Stage.Working(Step.DOWNLOAD, p, "${mb(done)} / ${if (total > 0) mb(total) else "?"}")
            }

            _stage.value = Stage.Working(Step.EXTRACT)
            val code = RootShell.stream(
                "${UbuPaths.script("install.sh")} ${Sh.quote(tarball.absolutePath)} ${release.sha256}",
                ::appendLog,
            )
            if (code != 0) error("install.sh failed (exit $code)")
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { downloadDir.deleteRecursively() }
        }

        // Keep what an earlier attempt (or a hand edit) left in the file.
        val previous = runCatching { ConfigStore.read() }.getOrDefault(UbuConfig())
        ConfigStore.write(getApplication(), previous.copy(defaultUser = req.user, aptMirror = req.mirror))

        _stage.value = Stage.Working(Step.SETUP)
        val args = buildString {
            append(UbuPaths.script("setup.sh")).append(" --user ").append(Sh.quote(req.user))
            if (req.nopasswd) append(" --nopasswd")
            if (req.password.isNotEmpty()) append(" --password-stdin")
        }
        // printf is a shell builtin, so the password never shows up in argv.
        val cmd = if (req.password.isNotEmpty()) "printf '%s\\n' ${Sh.quote(req.password)} | $args" else args
        val code = RootShell.stream(cmd, ::appendLog)
        if (code != 0) error("setup.sh failed (exit $code)")
    }

    /** Copies the picked archive into our files dir, where install.sh (root) reads it. */
    private suspend fun copyLocal(local: LocalArchive): File = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val app = getApplication<Application>()
        val resolver = app.contentResolver
        val total = resolver.query(local.uri, arrayOf(OpenableColumns.SIZE), null, null, null)?.use {
            if (it.moveToFirst() && !it.isNull(0)) it.getLong(0) else -1L
        } ?: -1L
        appendLog("local file: ${local.name}" + if (total > 0) " (${mb(total)})" else "")
        downloadDir.mkdirs()
        val out = File(downloadDir, LOCAL_NAME)
        val input = resolver.openInputStream(local.uri) ?: error("cannot open ${local.name}")
        input.use { src ->
            out.outputStream().use { dst ->
                val buf = ByteArray(1 shl 16)
                var done = 0L
                var shown = 0L
                while (true) {
                    val n = src.read(buf)
                    if (n < 0) break
                    dst.write(buf, 0, n)
                    done += n
                    if (done - shown >= 1 shl 20) {
                        shown = done
                        val p = if (total > 0) done.toFloat() / total else null
                        _stage.value = Stage.Working(Step.COPY, p, "${mb(done)} / ${if (total > 0) mb(total) else "?"}")
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        out
    }

    private fun appendLog(line: String) {
        _log.update { (it + line).takeLast(MAX_LOG_LINES) }
    }

    private fun mb(bytes: Long) = "%.1f MB".format(bytes / 1048576.0)

    companion object {
        const val MIN_FREE_BYTES = 3L * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
        private const val WAKE_LOCK_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000L
        private const val LOCAL_NAME = "local-rootfs.tar"

        private val SHA256 = Regex("[0-9a-fA-F]{64}")

        /** Empty (no check) or 64 hex digits, as install.sh accepts. */
        fun isValidSha256(s: String) = s.isEmpty() || SHA256.matches(s)
        private const val MAX_LOG_LINES = 1000
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/install/ReleaseDiscovery.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.install

import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

data class UbuntuRelease(val version: String, val fileName: String, val url: String, val sha256: String)

/**
 * Finds the newest Ubuntu Base LTS (YY.04 with even YY) for arm64 on
 * cdimage.ubuntu.com. Nothing is hardcoded: the directory listing, the
 * point release and the checksum all come from the server.
 */
object ReleaseDiscovery {
    const val BASE_URL = "https://cdimage.ubuntu.com/ubuntu-base/releases/"

    private val HREF = Regex("""href="([^"]+)"""")
    private val SERIES_DIR = Regex("""^(\d{2})\.04$""")

    /** Two-digit years of the LTS series in the releases index, newest first. */
    fun ltsSeries(indexHtml: String): List<Int> = hrefs(indexHtml)
        .mapNotNull { SERIES_DIR.matchEntire(lastSegment(it))?.groupValues?.get(1)?.toInt() }
        .filter { it % 2 == 0 }
        .distinct()
        .sortedDescending()

    /** The newest arm64 tarball of series [yy] in a `YY.04/release/` listing. */
    fun arm64Tarball(listingHtml: String, yy: Int): Pair<String, String>? {
        val re = Regex("""^ubuntu-base-(${"%02d".format(yy)}\.04(?:\.(\d+))?)-base-arm64\.tar\.gz$""")
        return hrefs(listingHtml)
            .mapNotNull { re.matchEntire(lastSegment(it)) }
            .maxByOrNull { it.groupValues[2].toIntOrNull() ?: 0 }
            ?.let { it.value to it.groupValues[1] }
    }

    /** SHA256SUMS lines look like `<hash> *<file>` (binary) or `<hash>  <file>`. */
    fun sha256For(sums: String, fileName: String): String? = sums.lineSequence()
        .map { it.trim().split(Regex("\\s+"), limit = 2) }
        .firstOrNull { it.size == 2 && it[1].removePrefix("*") == fileName }
        ?.get(0)
        ?.lowercase()
        ?.takeIf { it.matches(Regex("[0-9a-f]{64}")) }

    suspend fun discover(fetch: (String) -> String = Http::getText): UbuntuRelease = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val series = ltsSeries(fetch(BASE_URL))
        if (series.isEmpty()) error("no LTS release found at $BASE_URL")
        // A brand-new series directory may not have an arm64 build yet.
        for (yy in series.take(3)) {
            val dir = BASE_URL + "%02d.04/release/".format(yy)
            val listing = try {
                fetch(dir)
            } catch (e: HttpException) {
                if (e.code == 404) continue else throw e
            }
            val (file, version) = arm64Tarball(listing, yy) ?: continue
            val sha = sha256For(fetch(dir + "SHA256SUMS"), file)
                ?: error("$file is not listed in ${dir}SHA256SUMS")
            return@withContext UbuntuRelease(version, file, dir + file, sha)
        }
        error("no arm64 Ubuntu Base tarball found for LTS series $series")
    }

    private fun hrefs(html: String) = HREF.findAll(html).map { it.groupValues[1] }.toList()

    private fun lastSegment(href: String) = href.trimEnd('/').substringAfterLast('/')
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/install/UbuConfig.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.install

import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.Sh

/** uburoot.conf: shell variables sourced as root by every script. */
data class UbuConfig(
    val defaultUser: String = "",
    val mountSdcard: Boolean = true,
    val androidIntegration: Boolean = true,
    val dnsServers: String = DEFAULT_DNS,
    val initCommand: String = "",
    val hostname: String = "uburoot",
    /** Raised (never lowered) by mount.sh; global kernel setting. 0 = leave alone. */
    val inotifyMaxWatches: Int = DEFAULT_INOTIFY,
    /** Optional override of the /sdcard bind source (empty = auto-detect). */
    val sdcardSource: String = "",
    /** The root module's service.sh runs boot.sh at boot (mount + /etc/uburoot-boot.d). */
    val bootStart: Boolean = false,
    /** http:// Ubuntu ports mirror for the release suites (empty = ports.ubuntu.com); setup.sh. */
    val aptMirror: String = "",
    /** Kernel filesystems, ROM modules and uburoot-kernel inside Ubuntu (mount.sh). */
    val kernelAccess: Boolean = false,
    /** Device groups, permission rules, takeovers and uburoot-hw inside Ubuntu (mount.sh). */
    val hardwareAccess: Boolean = false,
    /** Keys this app version doesn't know (hand edits, newer versions): kept as they are. */
    val extra: Map<String, String> = emptyMap(),
) {
    /** Every value is single-quoted: the file is executed by a root shell. */
    fun render(): String = buildString {
        appendLine("# Written by the UbuRoot app. Sourced by the scripts as root.")
        line("MOUNT_SDCARD", if (mountSdcard) "1" else "0")
        line("ANDROID_INTEGRATION", if (androidIntegration) "1" else "0")
        line("DNS_SERVERS", dnsServers)
        line("DEFAULT_USER", defaultUser)
        line("INIT_COMMAND", initCommand)
        line("UBU_HOSTNAME", hostname)
        line("INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES", inotifyMaxWatches.coerceAtLeast(0).toString())
        if (sdcardSource.isNotEmpty()) line("SDCARD_SOURCE", sdcardSource)
        line("BOOT_START", if (bootStart) "1" else "0")
        if (aptMirror.isNotEmpty()) line("APT_MIRROR", aptMirror)
        line("KERNEL_ACCESS", if (kernelAccess) "1" else "0")
        line("HARDWARE_ACCESS", if (hardwareAccess) "1" else "0")
        for ((k, v) in extra) if (k !in KNOWN && SHELL_NAME.matches(k)) line(k, v)
    }

    private fun StringBuilder.line(key: String, value: String) {
        append(key).append('=').append(Sh.quote(value)).append('\n')
    }

    companion object {
        const val DEFAULT_DNS = "1.1.1.1 9.9.9.9"
        const val DEFAULT_INOTIFY = 524288

        private val USER_NAME = Regex("[a-z_][a-z0-9_-]{0,31}")
        private val SHELL_NAME = Regex("[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*")
        private val DNS_SERVER = Regex("[0-9a-fA-F.:]+")
        private val KNOWN = setOf(
            "MOUNT_SDCARD",
            "ANDROID_INTEGRATION",
            "DNS_SERVERS",
            "DEFAULT_USER",
            "INIT_COMMAND",
            "UBU_HOSTNAME",
            "INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES",
            "SDCARD_SOURCE",
            "BOOT_START",
            "APT_MIRROR",
            "KERNEL_ACCESS",
            "HARDWARE_ACCESS",
        )

        // Accounts that already exist in Ubuntu Base: setup.sh would reuse
        // them (and grant sudo) instead of creating a new user.
        private val RESERVED = setOf(
            "root", "daemon", "bin", "sys", "sync", "games", "man", "lp", "mail", "news",
            "uucp", "proxy", "www-data", "backup", "list", "irc", "_apt", "nobody",
        )

        fun isValidUserName(name: String) = USER_NAME.matches(name) && name !in RESERVED

        /** The same rule as setup.sh: at least one server, each only hex digits, dots and colons. */
        fun isValidDns(servers: String): Boolean {
            val list = servers.trim().split(Regex("\\s+")).filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
            return list.isNotEmpty() && list.all { DNS_SERVER.matches(it) }
        }

        /**
         * The same rule as `ubu_valid_mirror` in common.sh: plain http (Ubuntu Base has no
         * CA certificates before its first apt run), a host, an optional path, a final `/`.
         */
        fun isValidMirror(url: String) = MIRROR.matches(url)

        private val MIRROR = Regex("http://[A-Za-z0-9._~:-]+(/[A-Za-z0-9._~:-]+)*/")

        /** Reads a file written by [render]; missing keys get the scripts' defaults. */
        fun parse(text: String): UbuConfig {
            val map = ShellAssignments.parse(text)
            val d = UbuConfig()
            fun flag(key: String, default: Boolean) = map[key]?.let { it == "1" } ?: default
            return UbuConfig(
                defaultUser = map["DEFAULT_USER"] ?: d.defaultUser,
                mountSdcard = flag("MOUNT_SDCARD", d.mountSdcard),
                androidIntegration = flag("ANDROID_INTEGRATION", d.androidIntegration),
                dnsServers = map["DNS_SERVERS"]?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: d.dnsServers,
                initCommand = map["INIT_COMMAND"] ?: d.initCommand,
                hostname = map["UBU_HOSTNAME"]?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: d.hostname,
                inotifyMaxWatches = map["INOTIFY_MAX_WATCHES"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: d.inotifyMaxWatches,
                sdcardSource = map["SDCARD_SOURCE"] ?: d.sdcardSource,
                bootStart = flag("BOOT_START", d.bootStart),
                aptMirror = map["APT_MIRROR"] ?: d.aptMirror,
                kernelAccess = flag("KERNEL_ACCESS", d.kernelAccess),
                hardwareAccess = flag("HARDWARE_ACCESS", d.hardwareAccess),
                extra = map.filterKeys { it !in KNOWN },
            )
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Minimal reader for `KEY=value` lines as a POSIX shell would assign them:
 * single quotes, double quotes (with `\` escapes) and bare words, concatenated.
 * Enough for what [UbuConfig.render] writes and for simple hand edits; anything
 * else (commands, `$`) is kept literally and never executed here.
 */
object ShellAssignments {
    private val ASSIGNMENT = Regex("^\\s*(?:export\\s+)?([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)=(.*)$")

    fun parse(text: String): LinkedHashMap<String, String> {
        val out = LinkedHashMap<String, String>()
        for (line in text.lineSequence()) {
            val m = ASSIGNMENT.find(line) ?: continue
            out[m.groupValues[1]] = value(m.groupValues[2])
        }
        return out
    }

    private fun value(s: String): String {
        val b = StringBuilder()
        var i = 0
        while (i < s.length) {
            val c = s[i]
            when {
                c == '\'' -> {
                    val end = s.indexOf('\'', i + 1).let { if (it < 0) s.length else it }
                    b.append(s, i + 1, end)
                    i = end + 1
                }

                c == '"' -> {
                    i++
                    while (i < s.length && s[i] != '"') {
                        if (s[i] == '\\' && i + 1 < s.length && s[i + 1] in "\"\\$`") i++
                        b.append(s[i])
                        i++
                    }
                    i++
                }

                c == '\\' && i + 1 < s.length -> {
                    b.append(s[i + 1])
                    i += 2
                }

                c.isWhitespace() || c == ';' || c == '#' -> break

                else -> {
                    b.append(c)
                    i++
                }
            }
        }
        return b.toString()
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/kernel/KernelStatus.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.kernel

/** One recorded kernel change from kernel.sh: `change=TYPE NAME ORIGINAL`. */
data class KernelChange(val type: String, val name: String, val original: String)

/** Parsed output of `kernel.sh status` (key=value lines). */
data class KernelStatus(
    val access: Boolean,
    val selinux: String,
    val devMode: Boolean,
    val modulesLoaded: Int,
    /** kernel.kptr_restrict etc. as reported, in order. */
    val sysctls: List<Pair<String, String>>,
    val changes: List<KernelChange>,
) {
    val permissive get() = selinux == "permissive"

    companion object {
        fun parse(lines: List<String>): KernelStatus {
            var access = false
            var selinux = "absent"
            var devMode = false
            var modules = 0
            val sysctls = mutableListOf<Pair<String, String>>()
            val changes = mutableListOf<KernelChange>()
            for (line in lines) {
                val i = line.indexOf('=')
                if (i <= 0) continue
                val key = line.substring(0, i)
                val value = line.substring(i + 1)
                when {
                    key == "access" -> access = value == "on"

                    key == "selinux" -> selinux = value

                    key == "devmode" -> devMode = value == "on"

                    key == "modules_loaded" -> modules = value.toIntOrNull() ?: 0

                    key == "change" -> {
                        val parts = value.split(' ', limit = 3)
                        if (parts.size >= 2) changes += KernelChange(parts[0], parts[1], parts.getOrElse(2) { "" })
                    }

                    key.startsWith("kernel.") -> sysctls += key to value
                }
            }
            return KernelStatus(access, selinux, devMode, modules, sysctls, changes)
        }

        /** `kernel.sh module list`: "* name" = loaded through UbuRoot, "  name" = by Android. */
        fun parseModules(lines: List<String>): List<Pair<String, Boolean>> = lines.mapNotNull { l ->
            when {
                l.startsWith("* ") -> l.substring(2).trim() to true
                l.startsWith("  ") && l.isNotBlank() -> l.trim() to false
                else -> null
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/kernel/KernelViewModel.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.kernel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.RootShell
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.Sh
import com.reinanbr.uburoot.root.UbuPaths
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class KernelState(
    val loading: Boolean = true,
    val status: KernelStatus? = null,
    val modules: List<Pair<String, Boolean>> = emptyList(),
    val busy: Boolean = false,
    /** Output of the last action (kernel.sh's own messages). */
    val output: String = "",
    /** The kernel section of doctor.sh, once asked for. */
    val report: String? = null,
)

/**
 * The Kernel screen: everything goes through kernel.sh on the Android side, which
 * records each change for Revert / Stop all. The app asks for confirmation itself,
 * so the commands get -y.
 */
class KernelViewModel(app: Application) : AndroidViewModel(app) {
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(KernelState())
    val state: StateFlow<KernelState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private val kernel = UbuPaths.script("kernel.sh")

    init {
        refresh()
    }

    fun refresh() = viewModelScope.launch { reload() }

    private suspend fun reload() {
        val status = KernelStatus.parse(RootShell.run("$kernel status").out)
        val modules = KernelStatus.parseModules(RootShell.run("$kernel module list").out)
        _state.update { it.copy(loading = false, status = status, modules = modules) }
    }

    fun setDevMode(on: Boolean) = act("$kernel -y devmode ${if (on) "on" else "off"}")

    fun setPermissive(on: Boolean) = act("$kernel -y selinux ${if (on) "permissive" else "enforcing"}")

    fun unload(module: String) = act("$kernel -y module unload ${Sh.quote(module)}")

    fun revert() = act("$kernel -y revert")

    /** The Kernel section of the doctor report (read-only, a few seconds). */
    fun loadReport() = viewModelScope.launch {
        _state.update { it.copy(busy = true) }
        val text = RootShell.run(UbuPaths.script("doctor.sh")).out
        val section = text.dropWhile { it != "== Kernel ==" }.drop(1).takeWhile { !it.startsWith("== ") }
        _state.update { it.copy(busy = false, report = section.joinToString("\n").trim()) }
    }

    private fun act(cmd: String) {
        if (_state.value.busy) return
        _state.update { it.copy(busy = true, output = "") }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val out = try {
                RootShell.run(cmd).text
            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName
            }
            reload()
            _state.update { it.copy(busy = false, output = out.trim()) }
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/root/RootInfo.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.root

/** Parsed output of rootinfo.sh (`key=value` lines). */
data class RootInfo(private val values: Map<String, String>) {
    val impl get() = values["impl"].orEmpty().ifEmpty { "unknown" }
    val version get() = values["version"].orEmpty()
    val busybox get() = values["busybox"].orEmpty().ifEmpty { "none" }
    val arch get() = values["arch"].orEmpty()
    val freeBytes: Long? get() = values["free_kb"]?.toLongOrNull()?.times(1024)
    val rootfsInstalled get() = values["rootfs"] == "installed"
    val setupDone get() = values["setup"] == "done"
    val isArm64 get() = arch == "aarch64"

    /** Mounts are only visible to every session from the init mount namespace. */
    val globalNamespace: Boolean
        get() {
            val ns = values["mnt_ns"].orEmpty()
            return ns.isNotEmpty() && ns == values["init_mnt_ns"]
        }

    /**
     * UbuRoot requires root with full kernel access: every capability in the
     * effective and bounding sets, no seccomp filter, no no_new_privs
     * (`ubu_root_report` in common.sh). A root manager can hand out less, e.g. a
     * KernelSU App Profile with fewer capabilities. Older scripts don't report
     * it: then it is not checked.
     */
    val fullRoot: Boolean get() = values["full_root"] != "no"

    /** What the root gate saw, for the error screen. */
    val rootDetail: String
        get() = listOf("cap_eff", "cap_bnd", "seccomp", "no_new_privs", "selinux")
            .joinToString("\n") { "$it=${values[it].orEmpty()}" }

    /** Integration schema of the installed rootfs (/etc/uburoot-release; 0 = before it existed). */
    val schema: Int get() = values["schema"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 0

    /** The schema these scripts write (UBU_SCHEMA). */
    val schemaCurrent: Int get() = values["schema_current"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 0

    /** A set-up rootfs whose integration files are older than the scripts: `setup.sh --migrate`. */
    val needsMigration get() = rootfsInstalled && setupDone && schema < schemaCurrent

    companion object {
        fun parse(lines: List<String>): RootInfo = RootInfo(
            lines.mapNotNull { line ->
                val i = line.indexOf('=')
                if (i > 0) line.substring(0, i) to line.substring(i + 1) else null
            }.toMap(),
        )
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/root/RootShell.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.root

import com.topjohnwu.superuser.CallbackList
import com.topjohnwu.superuser.Shell
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/**
 * Thin coroutine wrapper over the libsu main shell. The default builder (see
 * UbuRootApp) asks for `su --mount-master`; libsu silently falls back to plain
 * `su` and then `sh`, so callers must check [RootInfo.globalNamespace].
 */
object RootShell {
    data class Result(val code: Int, val out: List<String>) {
        val ok get() = code == 0
        val text get() = out.joinToString("\n")
    }

    /**
     * Blocks until the root manager answers the grant prompt. libsu caches the
     * main shell, and after a denial that is the non-root `sh` fallback: drop
     * it so a retry asks `su` again.
     */
    suspend fun isRoot(): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        Shell.getCachedShell()?.let { if (!it.isRoot) it.close() }
        Shell.getShell().isRoot
    }

    /** Run [cmd] with stderr merged into stdout. */
    suspend fun run(cmd: String): Result = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val r = Shell.cmd(merged(cmd)).exec()
        Result(r.code, r.out)
    }

    /** Run [cmd], delivering each output line to [onLine] on the main thread. */
    suspend fun stream(cmd: String, onLine: (String) -> Unit): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val lines = object : CallbackList<String>() {
            override fun onAddElement(e: String) = onLine(e)
        }
        Shell.cmd(merged(cmd)).to(lines).exec().code
    }

    // A subshell, so `set -e`, `cd` or `exit` never leak into the shared shell.
    private fun merged(cmd: String) = "(\n$cmd\n) 2>&1"
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/reinanbr/uburoot/root/ScriptDeployer.kt`:

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.root

import android.content.Context
import java.io.File
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/** Copies assets/uburoot/bin to /data/local/uburoot/bin (root-owned, 0755). */
object ScriptDeployer {
    private const val ASSET_DIR = "uburoot/bin"

    suspend fun deploy(context: Context): RootShell.Result {
        val staging = File(context.cacheDir, "uburoot-bin")
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            staging.deleteRecursively()
            staging.mkdirs()
            for (name in context.assets.list(ASSET_DIR).orEmpty()) {
                context.assets.open("$ASSET_DIR/$name").use { input ->
                    File(staging, name).outputStream().use { input.copyTo(it) }
                }
            }
        }
        // Replace each script by rename, never in place: a running sh reads its
        // script incrementally, so truncating it under a live session breaks it.
        val src = Sh.quote(staging.absolutePath)
        val bin = UbuPaths.BIN
        return RootShell.run(
            """
            set -e
            mkdir -p $bin
            chmod 755 ${UbuPaths.BASE} $bin
            for f in $src/*; do
                n=${'$'}{f##*/}
                cmp -s "${'$'}f" "$bin/${'$'}n" 2>/dev/null && continue
                cp -f "${'$'}f" "$bin/.${'$'}n.new"
                chown 0:0 "$bin/.${'$'}n.new"
                chmod 755 "$bin/.${'$'}n.new"
                mv -f "$bin/.${'$'}n.new" "$bin/${'$'}n"
            done
            """.trimIndent(),
        ).also { withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { staging.deleteRecursively() } }
    }

    suspend fun rootInfo(): RootInfo = RootInfo.parse(RootShell.run(UbuPaths.script("rootinfo.sh")).out)
}
```

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.root

/** POSIX shell helpers for building commands that run through `su`. */
object Sh {
    /** Quote [s] as a single shell word: `it's` -> `'it'\''s'`. */
    fun quote(s: String): String = "'" + s.replace("'", "'\\''") + "'"
}
```

```kt
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
package com.reinanbr.uburoot.root

/** Device paths; keep in sync with common.sh and CLAUDE.md. */
object UbuPaths {
    const val BASE = "/data/local/uburoot"
    const val BIN = "$BASE/bin"
    const val ROOTFS = "$BASE/rootfs"
    const val CONFIG = "$BASE/uburoot.conf"

    fun script(name: String) = "$BIN/$name"
}
```
