`tests/shellcheck.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Run shellcheck (POSIX sh dialect) on every UbuRoot script.
# Uses shellcheck from PATH; otherwise the official koalaman/shellcheck image.
set -eu
ROOT=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)
# CI runners have their own shellcheck (0.9.0 on ubuntu-24.04); match it with
# SC_IMAGE=koalaman/shellcheck:v0.9.0 tests/shellcheck.sh
SC_IMAGE=${SC_IMAGE:-koalaman/shellcheck:v0.11.0}
cd "$ROOT"
set -- app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/*.sh app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/android-env \
    tests/*.sh tests/docker/*.sh tests/device/*.sh tools/*.sh \
    app/src/module/customize.sh app/src/module/service.sh app/src/module/META-INF/com/google/android/update-binary
if command -v shellcheck >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    shellcheck --version | sed -n 2p
    shellcheck -s sh -x -P app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin "$@"
elif command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "shellcheck not in PATH; using docker image $SC_IMAGE" >&2
    docker run --rm -v "$ROOT:/mnt:ro" -w /mnt "$SC_IMAGE" \
        -s sh -x -P app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin "$@"
else
    echo "shellcheck not found (apt install shellcheck)" >&2
    exit 2
fi
echo "shellcheck: OK ($# files)"

```

`tests/test_reset_guard.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Critical safety rule test, no root needed.
# Uses dummy directories, a fake mount table (UBU_MOUNTS_FILE) and a fake
# busybox whose umount can be sabotaged, to prove that reset.sh / ubu_safe_rm
# NEVER delete while anything is (or looks) mounted under the rootfs.
# safe_rm sources common.sh in a subshell on purpose (isolation).
# shellcheck disable=SC2030,SC2031
set -u
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
BIN=$(cd "$HERE/../app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin" && pwd)

T=$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/uburoot-guard.XXXXXX") || exit 1
trap 'rm -rf "$T"' EXIT INT TERM

PASS=0
FAIL=0
ok() { PASS=$((PASS + 1)); printf '  ok   %s\n' "$*"; }
bad() { FAIL=$((FAIL + 1)); printf '  FAIL %s\n' "$*"; }

# Fake busybox: umount/mount controlled by env, everything else passes through.
cat >"$T/fakebb" <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
case "$1" in
    umount) echo "fakebb: umount $*" >>"$FAKE_LOG"; exit "${FAKE_UMOUNT_RC:-1}" ;;
    mount) echo "fakebb: mount $*" >>"$FAKE_LOG"; exit 1 ;;
    *) exec "$@" ;;
esac
EOF
chmod +x "$T/fakebb"

export UBU_BASE="$T/base"
export ROOTFS="$T/base/rootfs"
export UBU_MOUNTS_FILE="$T/mounts"
export UBU_MOUNTINFO_FILE="$T/no-mountinfo"
export UBU_BB="$T/fakebb"
export UBU_ALLOW_NONROOT=1
export FAKE_LOG="$T/fakebb.log"

# Build a dummy rootfs where rootfs/data and rootfs/sdcard hold "phone data",
# exactly what they would expose if /data and /sdcard were bind-mounted.
make_rootfs() {
    rm -rf "$T/base"
    mkdir -p "$ROOTFS/usr/bin" "$ROOTFS/bin" "$ROOTFS/etc" "$ROOTFS/proc" "$ROOTFS/sys" "$ROOTFS/dev" \
        "$ROOTFS/data" "$ROOTFS/sdcard" "$T/base/keep"
    printf '#!/bin/sh\n' >"$ROOTFS/usr/bin/env"
    printf '#!/bin/sh\n' >"$ROOTFS/bin/bash"
    chmod +x "$ROOTFS/usr/bin/env" "$ROOTFS/bin/bash"
    echo "keep me" >"$T/base/keep/sibling.txt"
    : >"$UBU_MOUNTS_FILE"
}
phone_data() {
    echo "PRECIOUS photo" >"$ROOTFS/sdcard/DCIM.jpg"
    echo "PRECIOUS app db" >"$ROOTFS/data/app.db"
}
mounted() { printf '%s\n' "$@" >>"$UBU_MOUNTS_FILE"; }
precious_intact() { [ -f "$ROOTFS/sdcard/DCIM.jpg" ] && [ -f "$ROOTFS/data/app.db" ]; }

run_reset() { sh "$BIN/reset.sh" --yes >"$T/out" 2>&1; echo $?; }
# Call ubu_safe_rm directly, bypassing umount.sh.
safe_rm() {
    (
        # shellcheck source=app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin/common.sh
        . "$BIN/common.sh"
        ubu_init
        ubu_safe_rm "$1"
    ) >"$T/out" 2>&1
    echo $?
}

echo "== critical rule: reset must abort while mounts exist =="

make_rootfs
phone_data
mounted "/dev/block/dm-5 $ROOTFS/data f2fs rw 0 0" "/dev/fuse $ROOTFS/sdcard fuse rw 0 0"
export FAKE_UMOUNT_RC=1
rc=$(run_reset)
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && precious_intact; then ok "umount fails -> reset aborts (rc=3), data intact"; else bad "umount fails: rc=$rc"; cat "$T/out"; fi

make_rootfs
phone_data
mounted "/dev/block/dm-5 $ROOTFS/data f2fs rw 0 0"
export FAKE_UMOUNT_RC=0
rc=$(run_reset)
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && precious_intact && grep -q 'still mounted' "$T/out"; then
    ok "umount 'succeeds' but mount remains in /proc/mounts -> abort, data intact"
else
    bad "lying umount: rc=$rc"
    cat "$T/out"
fi

make_rootfs
phone_data
mounted "/dev/block/dm-5 $ROOTFS/data f2fs rw 0 0"
rc=$(safe_rm "$ROOTFS")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && precious_intact; then ok "ubu_safe_rm called directly while mounted -> refused"; else bad "direct safe_rm: rc=$rc"; fi

make_rootfs
phone_data
mounted "$ROOTFS $ROOTFS none rw,bind 0 0"
rc=$(safe_rm "$ROOTFS")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && precious_intact; then ok "self-bind on ROOTFS itself -> refused"; else bad "self-bind: rc=$rc"; fi

make_rootfs
phone_data
mounted "proc $ROOTFS/data/deep/nested/mount proc rw 0 0"
rc=$(safe_rm "$ROOTFS")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && precious_intact; then ok "deeply nested mount -> refused"; else bad "nested: rc=$rc"; fi

echo "== defense in depth (no mounts listed) =="

make_rootfs
phone_data
rc=$(safe_rm "$ROOTFS")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && precious_intact && grep -q 'not empty' "$T/out"; then
    ok "non-empty bind target (mount invisible here) -> refused by rmdir guard"
else
    bad "rmdir guard: rc=$rc"
fi

make_rootfs
mkdir -p "$ROOTFS/proc/self"
rc=$(safe_rm "$ROOTFS")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && [ -d "$ROOTFS" ]; then ok "proc/self marker -> refused"; else bad "marker: rc=$rc"; fi

# Kernel access (KERNEL_ACCESS=1): configfs with the USB gadget, cgroups and
# bpffs live under sys/fs and sys/kernel; the change log is bound at run/uburoot.
for m in sys/fs/cgroup sys/kernel/config/usb_gadget run/uburoot/state; do
    make_rootfs
    mkdir -p "$ROOTFS/$m"
    rc=$(safe_rm "$ROOTFS")
    if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && [ -d "$ROOTFS/$m" ]; then ok "$m marker -> refused"; else bad "$m marker: rc=$rc"; fi
done

make_rootfs
mkdir -p "$ROOTFS/usr/lib/modules/4.9.337/uburoot/vendor_lib_modules"
echo "vendor module" >"$ROOTFS/usr/lib/modules/4.9.337/uburoot/vendor_lib_modules/wlan.ko"
rc=$(safe_rm "$ROOTFS")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && [ -f "$ROOTFS/usr/lib/modules/4.9.337/uburoot/vendor_lib_modules/wlan.ko" ]; then
    ok "non-empty module bind target (vendor modules) -> refused"
else
    bad "module bind target: rc=$rc"
fi

make_rootfs
rc=$(safe_rm "$T")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && [ -d "$ROOTFS" ]; then ok "path outside UBU_BASE -> refused"; else bad "outside: rc=$rc"; fi

make_rootfs
rc=$(safe_rm "$UBU_BASE")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && [ -d "$ROOTFS" ]; then ok "UBU_BASE itself -> refused"; else bad "base: rc=$rc"; fi

make_rootfs
ln -s "$T/base/keep" "$T/base/link"
rc=$(safe_rm "$T/base/link")
if [ "$rc" = 3 ] && [ -f "$T/base/keep/sibling.txt" ]; then ok "symlink target -> refused"; else bad "symlink: rc=$rc"; fi

make_rootfs
rc=$(ROOTFS="$T/base/x/../rootfs" sh "$BIN/reset.sh" --yes >"$T/out" 2>&1; echo $?)
if [ "$rc" != 0 ] && [ -d "$ROOTFS" ]; then ok "ROOTFS with '..' -> rejected"; else bad "dotdot: rc=$rc"; fi

make_rootfs
rc=$(sh "$BIN/reset.sh" >"$T/out" 2>&1; echo $?)
if [ "$rc" != 0 ] && [ -d "$ROOTFS" ]; then ok "reset without --yes -> refused"; else bad "no --yes: rc=$rc"; fi

echo "== clean state: deletion is allowed =="

make_rootfs
mounted "/dev/block/dm-5 ${ROOTFS}2/data f2fs rw 0 0" "/dev/block/dm-5 ${ROOTFS}.old f2fs rw 0 0"
export FAKE_UMOUNT_RC=0
rc=$(run_reset)
if [ "$rc" = 0 ] && [ ! -e "$ROOTFS" ] && [ -f "$T/base/keep/sibling.txt" ]; then
    ok "no mounts under ROOTFS (similar-prefix mounts ignored) -> rootfs deleted, siblings kept"
else
    bad "clean reset: rc=$rc"
    cat "$T/out"
fi

echo
echo "reset guard: $PASS passed, $FAIL failed"
[ "$FAIL" = 0 ]

```

`tools/adb-push-scripts.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Push the UbuRoot scripts to a rooted device without the app:
#   tools/adb-push-scripts.sh [-s SERIAL]
# Then, for example:
#   adb shell su -M -c /data/local/uburoot/bin/rootinfo.sh
#   adb push ubuntu-base-*-arm64.tar.gz /data/local/tmp/
#   adb shell su -M -c "/data/local/uburoot/bin/install.sh /data/local/tmp/ubuntu-base-...tar.gz <sha256>"
#   adb shell su -M -c "/data/local/uburoot/bin/setup.sh --user ubuntu --nopasswd"
#   adb shell -t su -M -c /data/local/uburoot/bin/enter.sh
set -eu
ROOT=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)
SRC="$ROOT/app/src/main/assets/uburoot/bin"
TMP=/data/local/tmp/uburoot-bin
DEST=/data/local/uburoot/bin
ADB="adb"
if [ "${1:-}" = -s ] && [ -n "${2:-}" ]; then ADB="adb -s $2"; fi

$ADB shell "rm -rf $TMP && mkdir -p $TMP"
for f in "$SRC"/*; do
    $ADB push "$f" "$TMP/" >/dev/null
done
# --mount-master is accepted by Magisk, KernelSU and APatch (see CLAUDE.md).
$ADB shell "su --mount-master -c 'mkdir -p $DEST && cp $TMP/* $DEST/ && chmod 755 $DEST/* && chmod 644 $DEST/common.sh && chown -R 0:0 $DEST && rm -rf $TMP'"
$ADB shell "su --mount-master -c $DEST/rootinfo.sh"

```

`tools/ci-package.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Collects the built APKs into OUTDIR, each also as a "-module.zip" copy (same
# bytes: root managers' file pickers may not list *.apk), checks that every
# file is a signed APK that also carries the root-module files, and writes
# SHA256SUMS. Run after ./gradlew assembleDebug [assembleRelease].
# Tag builds also get version-less copies of the release (uburoot.apk,
# uburoot-module.zip), so releases/latest/download/<name> links never change.
# Usage: tools/ci-package.sh OUTDIR
set -eu
ROOT=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)
OUT=${1:?usage: ci-package.sh OUTDIR}
APKS="$ROOT/app/build/outputs/apk"

VERSION=$(sed -n 's/^val appVersionName = "\(.*\)"$/\1/p' "$ROOT/app/build.gradle.kts")
[ -n "$VERSION" ] || { echo "cannot read appVersionName" >&2; exit 1; }
# Tag builds are named after the version only; anything else gets the commit.
if [ "${GITHUB_REF_TYPE:-}" = tag ]; then
    NAME="uburoot-$VERSION"
else
    NAME="uburoot-$VERSION-g$(git -C "$ROOT" rev-parse --short HEAD)"
fi

# apksigner from the newest build-tools.
SDK=${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-}}
if [ -z "$SDK" ] && [ -f "$ROOT/local.properties" ]; then
    SDK=$(sed -n 's/^sdk\.dir=//p' "$ROOT/local.properties")
fi
APKSIGNER=$(for f in "$SDK"/build-tools/*/apksigner; do [ -x "$f" ] && echo "$f"; done | sort -V | tail -1)
[ -x "$APKSIGNER" ] || { echo "apksigner not found under $SDK/build-tools" >&2; exit 1; }

# What the module installers need at the APK root (see app/src/module).
MODULE_FILES="module.prop customize.sh service.sh META-INF/com/google/android/update-binary META-INF/com/google/android/updater-script"

check_apk() {
    "$APKSIGNER" verify "$1" || { echo "$1: signature does not verify" >&2; exit 1; }
    _list=$(unzip -Z1 "$1")
    for _f in $MODULE_FILES; do
        printf '%s\n' "$_list" | grep -qxF "$_f" || { echo "$1: missing $_f" >&2; exit 1; }
    done
    _prop=$(unzip -p "$1" module.prop)
    printf '%s\n' "$_prop" | grep -qx "version=$VERSION" || { echo "$1: module.prop version is not $VERSION" >&2; exit 1; }
    case "$_prop" in *@VERSION_*) echo "$1: unfilled placeholder in module.prop" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
}

mkdir -p "$OUT"
rm -f "$OUT"/uburoot*.apk "$OUT"/uburoot*-module.zip "$OUT/SHA256SUMS"

publish() { # publish SRC.apk FLAVOR
    check_apk "$1"
    cp "$1" "$OUT/$NAME-$2.apk"
    cp "$1" "$OUT/$NAME-$2-module.zip"
    echo "packaged $NAME-$2.apk (+ -module.zip)"
}

[ -f "$APKS/debug/app-debug.apk" ] || { echo "no debug APK; run assembleDebug first" >&2; exit 1; }
publish "$APKS/debug/app-debug.apk" debug
if [ -f "$APKS/release/app-release.apk" ]; then
    publish "$APKS/release/app-release.apk" release
    if [ "${GITHUB_REF_TYPE:-}" = tag ]; then
        cp "$APKS/release/app-release.apk" "$OUT/uburoot.apk"
        cp "$APKS/release/app-release.apk" "$OUT/uburoot-module.zip"
        echo "packaged uburoot.apk + uburoot-module.zip (stable names)"
    fi
elif [ -f "$APKS/release/app-release-unsigned.apk" ]; then
    echo "release APK is unsigned (no UBUROOT_KEYSTORE): not packaged"
fi

(cd "$OUT" && sha256sum uburoot*.apk uburoot*.zip >SHA256SUMS && cat SHA256SUMS)

```

`tools/fdroid-check.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
# Build one version the way F-Droid does and check it against the published
# release APK (reproducible build). Uses fdroidserver from PyPI in a temporary
# venv, the recipe tools/fdroid/com.reinanbr.uburoot.yml (the file submitted to
# fdroiddata) and the official category list.
# Usage: tools/fdroid-check.sh [VERSIONCODE]   (default: the recipe's CurrentVersionCode)
# Needs: python3, git, JDK 21 (JAVA_HOME), the Android SDK (ANDROID_HOME or
# ~/Android/Sdk). Run it after the version's tag and GitHub release exist.
set -eu
ROOT=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)
RECIPE="$ROOT/tools/fdroid/com.reinanbr.uburoot.yml"
APP=com.reinanbr.uburoot
VC=${1:-$(awk '/^CurrentVersionCode:/ { print $2 }' "$RECIPE")}
SDK=${ANDROID_HOME:-$HOME/Android/Sdk}
WORK=$(mktemp -d)
trap 'rm -rf "$WORK"' EXIT

# The Gradle version the wrapper asks for; the wrapper checks its SHA-256
# (distributionSha256Sum, the same value as F-Droid's gradle-transparency-log).
(cd "$ROOT" && ./gradlew -q --version >/dev/null)
GV=$(sed -n 's/^distributionUrl=.*gradle-\(.*\)-bin\.zip$/\1/p' "$ROOT/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties")
GRADLE=$(find "$HOME/.gradle/wrapper/dists/gradle-$GV-bin" -path "*/gradle-$GV/bin/gradle" -type f | head -n 1)
[ -n "$GRADLE" ] || { echo "Gradle $GV not found in the wrapper cache" >&2; exit 1; }

python3 -m venv "$WORK/venv"
"$WORK/venv/bin/pip" install -q fdroidserver
F="$WORK/fdroiddata"
mkdir -p "$F/metadata" "$F/config" "$F/build"
cp "$RECIPE" "$F/metadata/"
# Category icons are not needed for lint.
curl -fsSL https://gitlab.com/fdroid/fdroiddata/-/raw/master/config/categories.yml |
    sed '/^ *icon:/d' >"$F/config/categories.yml"
printf 'sdk_path: %s\ngradle: %s\n' "$SDK" "$GRADLE" >"$F/config.yml"
chmod 600 "$F/config.yml"
# fdroidserver's local mode reads the source date before it clones.
git clone -q "$(awk '/^Repo:/ { print $2 }' "$RECIPE")" "$F/build/$APP"

cd "$F"
"$WORK/venv/bin/fdroid" lint "$APP"
"$WORK/venv/bin/fdroid" rewritemeta "$APP"
if ! cmp -s "$RECIPE" "metadata/$APP.yml"; then
    echo "fdroid rewritemeta would change the recipe:" >&2
    diff "$RECIPE" "metadata/$APP.yml" >&2 || true
    exit 1
fi
"$WORK/venv/bin/fdroid" build -l --no-tarball "$APP:$VC" >"$WORK/build.log" 2>&1 || true
if grep -q 'compared built binary to supplied reference binary successfully' "$WORK/build.log"; then
    echo "fdroid build $APP:$VC: built from source and identical to the published APK"
    exit 0
fi
tail -n 40 "$WORK/build.log" >&2
echo "fdroid build $APP:$VC: FAILED (log above)" >&2
exit 1

```
